      0, 0, 0, kIOUCVariableStructureSize },
    /* kRTW88PowerOn */
    { (IOExternalMethodAction)&RTW88UserClient::sPowerOn,
      0, 0, 0, 0 },
    /* kRTW88PowerOff */
    { (IOExternalMethodAction)&RTW88UserClient::sPowerOff,
      0, 0, 0, 0 },
};

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/*  Factory                                                             */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

RTW88UserClient *RTW88UserClient::create(RTW88PCIDevice *dev, task_t owningTask)
{
    RTW88UserClient *uc = new RTW88UserClient;
    if (uc && !uc->init(nullptr)) { uc->release(); return nullptr; }
    if (uc) { uc->_provider = dev; uc->_owningTask = owningTask; }
    return uc;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/*  IOService lifecycle                                                 */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

bool RTW88UserClient::init(OSDictionary *props)
{
    return super::init(props);
}

bool RTW88UserClient::initWithTask(task_t owningTask, void *securityID, UInt32 type, OSDictionary *properties)
{
    _owningTask = owningTask;
    if (!super::initWithTask(owningTask, securityID, type, properties)) {
        IOLog("rtw88: RTW88UserClient::initWithTask(4) super failed\n");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool RTW88UserClient::initWithTask(task_t owningTask, void *securityID, UInt32 type)
{
    _owningTask = owningTask;
    if (!super::initWithTask(owningTask, securityID, type)) {
        IOLog("rtw88: RTW88UserClient::initWithTask(3) super failed\n");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool RTW88UserClient::start(IOService *provider)
{
    if (!super::start(provider)) {
        IOLog("rtw88: RTW88UserClient::start() super::start failed\n");
        return false;
    }
    _provider = OSDynamicCast(RTW88PCIDevice, provider);
    if (!_provider) {
        IOLog("rtw88: RTW88UserClient::start() OSDynamicCast to RTW88PCIDevice failed\n");
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void RTW88UserClient::stop(IOService *provider)
{
    super::stop(provider);
}

void RTW88UserClient::free()
{
    super::free();
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::clientClose()
{
    terminate();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/*  externalMethod dispatch                                             */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::externalMethod(uint32_t selector,
                                          IOExternalMethodArguments *args,
                                          IOExternalMethodDispatch *dispatch,
                                          OSObject *target, void *reference)
{
    if (selector >= kRTW88NumSelectors)
        return kIOReturnUnsupported;

    const IOExternalMethodDispatch *d = &sMethods[selector];
    return super::externalMethod(selector, args,
                                  const_cast<IOExternalMethodDispatch *>(d),
                                  this, nullptr);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/*  Individual selectors                                                */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sScan(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                  IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) return kIOReturnOffline;
    return uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdScan();
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sConnect(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                     IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) return kIOReturnOffline;
    if (!args->structureInput || args->structureInputSize < sizeof(RTW88ConnectArgs))
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    const RTW88ConnectArgs *ca = (const RTW88ConnectArgs *)args->structureInput;
    return uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdConnect(ca->ssid, ca->password);
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sDisconnect(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                        IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) return kIOReturnOffline;
    return uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdDisconnect();
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sGetState(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                      IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!args->structureOutput || args->structureOutputSize < sizeof(RTW88StateResult))
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    RTW88StateResult *result = (RTW88StateResult *)args->structureOutput;
    memset(result, 0, sizeof(*result));

    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) {
        strlcpy(result->chip_name, "Uninitialized", sizeof(result->chip_name));
        args->structureOutputSize = sizeof(*result);
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    }

    IOReturn ret = uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdGetState(result);
    if (ret != kIOReturnSuccess) return ret;

    args->structureOutputSize = sizeof(*result);
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sGetBSSList(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                        IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!args->structureOutput) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) {
        args->structureOutputSize = 0;
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    }

    uint32_t len = (uint32_t)args->structureOutputSize;
    IOReturn ret = uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdGetBSSList(
        (uint8_t *)args->structureOutput, &len);
    args->structureOutputSize = len;
    return ret;
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sGetRSSI(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                     IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!args->scalarOutput || args->scalarOutputCount < 1) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) {
        args->scalarOutput[0] = (uint64_t)(int64_t)-100;
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    }
    int rssi = -100;
    IOReturn ret = uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdGetRSSI(&rssi);
    args->scalarOutput[0] = (uint64_t)(int64_t)rssi;
    return ret;
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sSetDebug(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                      IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (args->scalarInputCount >= 1) {
        extern int rtw88_log_level;
        rtw88_log_level = (int)args->scalarInput[0];
    }
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

extern "C" {
    uint32_t rtw88_read_log(char *out_buf, uint32_t max_len);
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sGetLog(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                    IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!args->structureOutput || args->structureOutputSize == 0)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    uint32_t max_len = (uint32_t)args->structureOutputSize;
    uint32_t read = rtw88_read_log((char *)args->structureOutput, max_len);
    
    args->structureOutputSize = read;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sPowerOn(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                   IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) return kIOReturnOffline;
    return uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdPowerOn();
}

IOReturn RTW88UserClient::sPowerOff(RTW88UserClient *uc, void *ref,
                                    IOExternalMethodArguments *args)
{
    if (!uc->_provider || !uc->_provider->get80211()) return kIOReturnOffline;
    return uc->_provider->get80211()->cmdPowerOff();
}

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
 * Private Sequoia WCL -> RTW88 radio control bridge.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLRadioBridge.hpp"
#include "RTW88UserClient.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <string.h>

RTW88WCLRadioBridge::~RTW88WCLRadioBridge()
{
    detach();
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::attach(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio,
                                     RTW88WCLRadioEventSink *sink)
{
    if (!radio || !sink)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (_radio)
        return kIOReturnBusy;
    if (radio->_delegate && radio->_delegate != this)
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    _radio = radio;
    _sink = sink;
    __atomic_store_n(&_scanInFlight, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_externalSupplicant, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    _radio->setEventDelegate(this);
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

void RTW88WCLRadioBridge::detach()
{
    RTW88IEEE80211 *radio = _radio;
    _sink = nullptr;
    __atomic_store_n(&_scanInFlight, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_externalSupplicant, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);

    /* The backend exposes its current delegate because it historically had
     * one owning controller. Do not erase a delegate installed by a newer
     * frontend instance. */
    if (radio && radio->_delegate == this)
        radio->setEventDelegate(nullptr);
    _radio = nullptr;
}

bool RTW88WCLRadioBridge::isZeroAddress(const uint8_t address[6])
{
    if (!address) return true;
    uint8_t aggregate = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 6; ++i) aggregate |= address[i];
    return aggregate == 0;
}

bool RTW88WCLRadioBridge::isValidBSSID(const uint8_t address[6])
{
    return address && !isZeroAddress(address) && !(address[0] & 0x01);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::startScan(const uint16_t *channels,
                                        uint32_t channelCount,
                                        uint32_t *generationOut)
{
    if (generationOut) *generationOut = 0;
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if ((channelCount && !channels) || channelCount > 128)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (__atomic_load_n(&_scanInFlight, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0)
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    /* Filter malformed channel hints but preserve the caller's order. The
     * RTW88 backend performs the hardware/regulatory capability selection. */
    uint16_t normalized[128] = {};
    uint32_t normalizedCount = 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < channelCount; ++i) {
        const uint16_t channel = channels[i];
        if (channel == 0 || channel > 196)
            return kIOReturnBadArgument;
        bool duplicate = false;
        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < normalizedCount; ++j) {
            if (normalized[j] == channel) {
                duplicate = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!duplicate)
            normalized[normalizedCount++] = channel;
    }

    /* Publish the transaction before asking the backend to start it. The
     * backend is asynchronous, but it may complete quickly; publishing after
     * cmdScan() returned would allow a completion callback to be lost and
     * leave the bridge stuck in scan-in-flight state. */
    const uint32_t generation =
        __atomic_add_fetch(&_scanGeneration, 1U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
    if (generationOut) *generationOut = generation;
    __atomic_store_n(&_scanInFlight, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);

    const IOReturn status = _radio->cmdScan(
        normalizedCount ? normalized : nullptr, normalizedCount);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess &&
        __atomic_load_n(&_scanGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == generation)
        __atomic_store_n(&_scanInFlight, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    return status;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::abortScan()
{
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (!__atomic_load_n(&_scanInFlight, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
        return kIOReturnSuccess;

    const IOReturn status = _radio->cmdAbortScan();
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess)
        return status;

    /* scanDone() normally clears the flag and emits the completion event.
     * A successful backend abort can also reach its bounded forced-idle path;
     * in that case synthesize exactly one bridge-level completion. */
    RTW88StateResult state = {};
    if (_radio->cmdGetState(&state) == kIOReturnSuccess &&
        state.state != RTW88_STATE_SCANNING &&
        __atomic_exchange_n(&_scanInFlight, 0U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL)) {
        RTW88WCLRadioEvent event = {};
        event.event = kRTW88EventScanDone;
        event.value = 1; // synthesized completion means aborted
        event.scanGeneration = __atomic_load_n(&_scanGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
        if (_sink) _sink->onRTW88WCLRadioEvent(event);
    }
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::associate(const uint8_t *ssid,
                                        uint8_t ssidLength,
                                        const uint8_t *bssidOrNull,
                                        const uint8_t *pmkOrNull,
                                        const uint8_t *rsnIEOrNull,
                                        uint16_t rsnIELength,
                                        bool externalSupplicant,
                                        uint32_t *generationOut)
{
    if (generationOut) *generationOut = 0;
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (!ssid || ssidLength == 0 || ssidLength > 32)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (bssidOrNull && !isZeroAddress(bssidOrNull) && !isValidBSSID(bssidOrNull))
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if ((rsnIELength && !rsnIEOrNull) || rsnIELength > 512)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (rsnIELength) {
        if (rsnIELength < 2 || rsnIEOrNull[0] != 48 ||
            static_cast<uint16_t>(rsnIEOrNull[1] + 2U) != rsnIELength)
            return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    }

    char ssidString[33] = {};
    if (memchr(ssid, 0, ssidLength) != nullptr)
        return kIOReturnUnsupported; // backend still uses a C-string SSID
    memcpy(ssidString, ssid, ssidLength);
    ssidString[ssidLength] = '\0';

    const uint8_t *bssid = isZeroAddress(bssidOrNull) ? nullptr : bssidOrNull;

    RTW88StateResult state = {};
    if (_radio->cmdGetState(&state) == kIOReturnSuccess &&
        (state.state == RTW88_STATE_AUTHENTICATING ||
         state.state == RTW88_STATE_ASSOCIATING ||
         state.state == RTW88_STATE_HANDSHAKING ||
         state.state == RTW88_STATE_CONNECTED)) {
        if (_radio->matchesPendingJoin(ssidString, bssid, pmkOrNull)) {
            if (generationOut)
                *generationOut = __atomic_load_n(&_joinGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
            return kIOReturnSuccess;
        }
        const IOReturn disconnectStatus = _radio->cmdDisconnect();
        if (disconnectStatus != kIOReturnSuccess)
            return disconnectStatus;
    }

    if (rsnIELength) {
        const IOReturn rsnStatus = _radio->cmdSetAssocRsnIE(rsnIEOrNull, rsnIELength);
        if (rsnStatus != kIOReturnSuccess)
            return rsnStatus;
    } else {
        _radio->clearAssocRsnIE();
    }

    const uint32_t protectedAssociation = (rsnIELength != 0 || pmkOrNull != nullptr) ? 1U : 0U;
    __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, protectedAssociation, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);

    /* cmdConnect() schedules authentication asynchronously and can emit a
     * backend event before returning to us. Publish the join generation and
     * pending flag first so that the event is associated with this request. */
    const uint32_t generation =
        __atomic_add_fetch(&_joinGeneration, 1U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
    if (generationOut) *generationOut = generation;
    __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    __atomic_store_n(&_externalSupplicant, externalSupplicant ? 1U : 0U,
                     __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    const IOReturn status = _radio->cmdConnect(
        ssidString, nullptr, pmkOrNull, bssid, externalSupplicant);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess &&
        __atomic_load_n(&_joinGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == generation) {
        __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        __atomic_store_n(&_externalSupplicant, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    }
    bzero(ssidString, sizeof(ssidString));
    return status;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::abortJoin()
{
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    const IOReturn status = _radio->cmdDisconnect();
    if (status == kIOReturnSuccess) {
        __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        __atomic_store_n(&_externalSupplicant, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    }
    return status;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::leaveNetwork()
{
    return abortJoin();
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::setAssociationRSNIE(const uint8_t *ie,
                                                  uint16_t length)
{
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (!ie || length < 2 || length > 512 || ie[0] != 48 ||
        static_cast<uint16_t>(ie[1] + 2U) != length)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    const IOReturn status = _radio->cmdSetAssocRsnIE(ie, length);
    if (status == kIOReturnSuccess)
        __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    return status;
}

void RTW88WCLRadioBridge::clearAssociationRSNIE()
{
    if (_radio) _radio->clearAssocRsnIE();
    __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
}

bool RTW88WCLRadioBridge::connectionReady()
{
    if (!_radio)
        return false;
    RTW88StateResult state = {};
    if (_radio->cmdGetState(&state) != kIOReturnSuccess ||
        state.state != RTW88_STATE_CONNECTED)
        return false;
    const bool external =
        __atomic_load_n(&_externalSupplicant, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0;
    return !external || !associationProtected() || _radio->externalKeysReady();
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::installKey(bool pairwise, uint8_t keyIndex,
                                         uint32_t keyCipher,
                                         const uint8_t *key,
                                         uint8_t keyLength)
{
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (!key || keyLength == 0)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    return _radio->cmdInstallExternalKey(pairwise, keyIndex, keyCipher,
                                         key, keyLength);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::copyScanBSS(uint32_t index, RTW88BSS *out)
{
    return _radio ? _radio->copyScanBSS(index, out) : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::copyCurrentBSS(RTW88BSS *out)

    return _radio ? _radio->copyCurrentBSS(out) : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::copyChannels(RTW88Channel *out,
                                           uint32_t capacity,
                                           uint32_t *count)
{
    return _radio ? _radio->copyChannels(out, capacity, count) : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::getRSSI(int *rssi)
{
    return _radio ? _radio->cmdGetRSSI(rssi) : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::getState(RTW88StateResult *state)
{
    return _radio ? _radio->cmdGetState(state) : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::powerOn()
{
    return _radio ? _radio->cmdPowerOn() : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::powerOff()
{
    return _radio ? _radio->cmdPowerOff() : kIOReturnNotReady;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::setMACAddress(const uint8_t address[6])
{
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (!address) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    return _radio->setMACAddress(address);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLRadioBridge::getMACAddress(uint8_t address[6])
{
    if (!_radio) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (!address) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    _radio->getMACAddress(address);
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

UInt32 RTW88WCLRadioBridge::outputEthernetPacket(mbuf_t packet)
{
    return _radio ? _radio->outputPacket(packet) : static_cast<UInt32>(kIOReturnNotReady);
}

void RTW88WCLRadioBridge::rtw88Event(RTW88Event eventType, void *data)
{
    RTW88WCLRadioEvent event = {};
    event.event = eventType;
    event.scanGeneration = __atomic_load_n(&_scanGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
    event.joinGeneration = __atomic_load_n(&_joinGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
    if (data && (eventType == kRTW88EventScanDone ||
                 eventType == kRTW88EventDeauth))
        event.value = *static_cast<const uint32_t *>(data);

    switch (eventType) {
    case kRTW88EventScanDone:
        /* Suppress a late completion after abortScan() already synthesized
         * the completion for a backend forced-idle path. */
        if (!__atomic_exchange_n(&_scanInFlight, 0U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL))
            return;
        break;
    case kRTW88EventAssocDone:
        /* This is the raw 802.11 association boundary. For WPA2/WCL the
         * controlled port is still closed until Apple installs PTK and GTK;
         * the WCL interface must check externalKeysReady() before reporting
         * the full connection as usable. */
        __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        break;
    case kRTW88EventDisconnected:
        __atomic_store_n(&_joinPending, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        __atomic_store_n(&_associationProtected, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        __atomic_store_n(&_externalSupplicant, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        break;
    case kRTW88EventDeauth:
    case kRTW88EventRSSIChanged:
    case kRTW88EventConnected:
        /* Delivered to the WCL event queue; connectionReady() queries the
         * backend state before the interface publishes link-up. */
        break;
    }

    RTW88WCLRadioEventSink *sink = _sink;
    if (sink)
        sink->onRTW88WCLRadioEvent(event);
}

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
 * RTL88WiFi private Sequoia WCL development layer.
 *
 * This bridge is deliberately independent of Apple's private WCL classes.
 * It translates the core infrastructure operations needed by a future
 * IO80211InfraProtocol frontend into the already-tested RTW88IEEE80211 API.
 * The WCL controller/interface owns ABI dispatch, message posting and
 * Skywalk packet lifetime; this class owns none of those objects.
 */
#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <sys/mbuf.h>
#include <IOKit/IOReturn.h>

#include "RTW88IEEE80211.hpp"

struct RTW88StateResult;

struct RTW88WCLRadioEvent {
    RTW88Event event;
    uint32_t scanGeneration;
    uint32_t joinGeneration;
    uint32_t value;
};

class RTW88WCLRadioEventSink {
public:
    /* Implementations must enqueue the event onto the native WCL work queue.
     * Do not call IO80211Controller::postMessage from the RTW88 callback's
     * arbitrary execution context. */
    virtual void onRTW88WCLRadioEvent(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event) = 0;
    virtual ~RTW88WCLRadioEventSink() {}
};

class RTW88WCLRadioBridge final : public RTW88EventDelegate {
public:
    RTW88WCLRadioBridge() = default;
    ~RTW88WCLRadioBridge() override;

    RTW88WCLRadioBridge(const RTW88WCLRadioBridge &) = delete;
    RTW88WCLRadioBridge &operator=(const RTW88WCLRadioBridge &) = delete;

    /* Non-owning attachment. The native frontend must own an otherwise
     * unclaimed radio event delegate; refusing to replace an existing
     * delegate prevents an experimental WCL instance from silently stealing
     * callbacks from the currently-running legacy frontend. Stop/quiesce the
     * radio before detach/destruction so no callback can still be in flight. */
    IOReturn attach(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio, RTW88WCLRadioEventSink *sink);
    void detach();
    bool isAttached() const { return _radio != nullptr; }

    IOReturn startScan(const uint16_t *channels, uint32_t channelCount,
                        uint32_t *generationOut = nullptr);
    IOReturn abortScan();

    /* WCL SSIDs are length-delimited. The current radio backend accepts a
     * NUL-terminated string, so embedded-NUL SSIDs are rejected rather than
     * silently truncated until the backend itself is converted to a
     * length-delimited representation. A null/all-zero BSSID means wildcard. */
    IOReturn associate(const uint8_t *ssid, uint8_t ssidLength,
                       const uint8_t *bssidOrNull,
                       const uint8_t *pmkOrNull,
                       const uint8_t *rsnIEOrNull,
                       uint16_t rsnIELength,
                       bool externalSupplicant = false,
                       uint32_t *generationOut = nullptr);
    IOReturn abortJoin();
    IOReturn leaveNetwork();
    IOReturn setAssociationRSNIE(const uint8_t *ie, uint16_t length);
    void clearAssociationRSNIE();

    /* True once the backend is in CONNECTED state. When the optional external
     * supplicant path is selected, protected links also require PTK+GTK; native
     * WCL currently uses the backend's internal WPA2 handshake instead. Raw
     * AssocDone alone does not mean the controlled port is open. */
    bool connectionReady();
    bool associationProtected() const {
        return __atomic_load_n(&_associationProtected, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0;
    }

    /* keyCipher must already be translated by the WCL frontend to the Linux
     * WLAN_CIPHER_SUITE_* value expected by RTW88IEEE80211. */
    IOReturn installKey(bool pairwise, uint8_t keyIndex, uint32_t keyCipher,
                        const uint8_t *key, uint8_t keyLength);

    IOReturn copyScanBSS(uint32_t index, RTW88BSS *out);
    IOReturn copyCurrentBSS(RTW88BSS *out);
    IOReturn copyChannels(RTW88Channel *out, uint32_t capacity, uint32_t *count);
    IOReturn getRSSI(int *rssi);
    IOReturn powerOn();
    IOReturn powerOff();
    IOReturn setMACAddress(const uint8_t address[6]);
    IOReturn getMACAddress(uint8_t address[6]);
    IOReturn getState(RTW88StateResult *state);

    /* This is only the backend hand-off. A Skywalk caller must translate its
     * packet to an mbuf with correct ownership before calling it, and must
     * preserve the backend's OUTPUT_STALL / OUTPUT_DROPPED semantics. */
    UInt32 outputEthernetPacket(mbuf_t packet);

    uint32_t scanGeneration() const {
        return __atomic_load_n(&_scanGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
    }
    uint32_t joinGeneration() const {
        return __atomic_load_n(&_joinGeneration, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
    }
    bool scanInFlight() const {
        return __atomic_load_n(&_scanInFlight, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0;
    }
    bool joinPending() const {
        return __atomic_load_n(&_joinPending, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0;
    }
    bool externalKeysReady() const {
        return _radio && _radio->externalKeysReady();
    }

    void rtw88Event(RTW88Event event, void *data) override;

private:
    static bool isZeroAddress(const uint8_t address[6]);
    static bool isValidBSSID(const uint8_t address[6]);

    RTW88IEEE80211 *_radio = nullptr;                 // non-owning
    RTW88WCLRadioEventSink *_sink = nullptr;           // non-owning
    uint32_t _scanGeneration = 0;   // accessed with __atomic builtins
    uint32_t _joinGeneration = 0;   // accessed with __atomic builtins
    uint32_t _scanInFlight = 0;     // accessed with __atomic builtins
    uint32_t _joinPending = 0;      // accessed with __atomic builtins
    uint32_t _associationProtected = 0; // accessed with __atomic builtins
    uint32_t _externalSupplicant = 0;   // accessed with __atomic builtins
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
 * Fixed-capacity event ring shared by the private WCL event dispatcher.
 * This header is deliberately independent of IOKit so its queue semantics
 * can be tested on the host toolchain.
 */
#pragma once

#include <stddef.h>

/* Single-owner ring: callers must provide external synchronization when the
 * producer and consumer can run concurrently. No allocation occurs after
 * construction, and enqueue/dequeue are O(1). */
template <typename T, size_t Capacity>
class RTW88WCLRadioEventRing final {
    static_assert(Capacity > 1, "event ring must have at least two slots");

public:
    bool push(const T &value)
    {
        if (_count == Capacity)
            return false;
        _items[_tail] = value;
        _tail = next(_tail);
        ++_count;
        return true;
    }

    bool pop(T *out)
    {
        if (!out || _count == 0)
            return false;
        *out = _items[_head];
        _head = next(_head);
        --_count;
        return true;
    }

    /* Replace the newest slot only when it matches. Restricting coalescing
     * to adjacent telemetry preserves ordering around scan/link transitions. */
    template <typename Predicate>
    bool replaceLast(Predicate predicate, const T &value)
    {
        if (_count == 0)
            return false;
        const size_t index = (_tail + Capacity - 1) % Capacity;
        if (!predicate(_items[index]))
            return false;
        _items[index] = value;
        return true;
    }

    /* Remove a logical (head-relative) slot while preserving FIFO order. */
    bool removeAt(size_t offset)
    {
        if (offset >= _count)
            return false;
        for (size_t i = offset; i + 1 < _count; ++i) {
            const size_t dst = (_head + i) % Capacity;
            const size_t src = (_head + i + 1) % Capacity;
            _items[dst] = _items[src];
        }
        _tail = (_tail + Capacity - 1) % Capacity;
        --_count;
        return true;
    }

    template <typename Predicate>
    bool removeFirst(Predicate predicate)
    {
        for (size_t offset = 0; offset < _count; ++offset) {
            const size_t index = (_head + offset) % Capacity;
            if (predicate(_items[index]))
                return removeAt(offset);
        }
        return false;
    }

    void clear()
    {
        _head = 0;
        _tail = 0;
        _count = 0;
    }

    size_t size() const { return _count; }
    bool empty() const { return _count == 0; }
    bool full() const { return _count == Capacity; }

private:
    static size_t next(size_t index) { return (index + 1) % Capacity; }

    T _items[Capacity] = {};
    size_t _head = 0;
    size_t _tail = 0;
    size_t _count = 0;
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
 * Serialized RTW88 -> native WCL event delivery.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLWorkQueue.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>

#define super IOEventSource
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLWorkQueue, IOEventSource)

bool RTW88WCLWorkQueue::init(OSObject *owner, IOWorkLoop *workLoop,
                             RTW88WCLRadioEventHandler *handler)
{
    if (!owner || !workLoop || !handler || _lock || _workLoop)
        return false;
    if (!super::init(owner, nullptr))
        return false;

    _lock = IOSimpleLockAlloc();
    if (!_lock)
        return false;

    _workLoop = workLoop;
    _handler = handler;
    _events.clear();
    _pendingSignal = 0;
    _stopping = 0;
    _registered = 0;
    _dropped = 0;
    _coalescedRSSI = 0;

    const IOReturn status = workLoop->addEventSource(this);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        _workLoop = nullptr;
        _handler = nullptr;
        IOSimpleLockFree(_lock);
        _lock = nullptr;
        return false;
    }
    _registered = 1;
    return true;
}

void RTW88WCLWorkQueue::stop()
{
    IOSimpleLock *lock = _lock;
    if (!lock)
        return;

    IOInterruptState interruptState = IOSimpleLockLockDisableInterrupt(lock);
    _stopping = 1;
    _handler = nullptr;
    _events.clear();
    _pendingSignal = 0;
    IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, interruptState);

    /* Caller must have detached/quiesced the RTW88 event producer. Removal is
     * synchronous with the IOWorkLoop and waits out an active checkForWork(). */
    if (_registered && _workLoop) {
        _workLoop->removeEventSource(this);
        _registered = 0;
    }
    _workLoop = nullptr;
}

void RTW88WCLWorkQueue::free()
{
    stop();
    if (_lock) {
        IOSimpleLockFree(_lock);
        _lock = nullptr;
    }
    super::free();
}

bool RTW88WCLWorkQueue::isTelemetryEvent(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event)
{
    return event.event == kRTW88EventRSSIChanged;
}

bool RTW88WCLWorkQueue::enqueueLocked(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event)
{
    if (isTelemetryEvent(event)) {
        const bool replaced = _events.replaceLast(
            [&event](const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &queued) {
                return queued.event == kRTW88EventRSSIChanged &&
                       queued.joinGeneration == event.joinGeneration;
            }, event);
        if (replaced) {
            ++_coalescedRSSI;
            return true;
        }
    }

    if (_events.full() && !isTelemetryEvent(event)) {
        /* RSSI samples are advisory; preserve scan/association/disconnect
         * transitions by evicting the oldest RSSI sample first. */
        const bool evicted = _events.removeFirst(
            [](const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &queued) {
                return queued.event == kRTW88EventRSSIChanged;
            });
        if (evicted)
            ++_dropped;
    }

    if (!_events.push(event)) {
        ++_dropped;
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void RTW88WCLWorkQueue::onRTW88WCLRadioEvent(
    const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event)
{
    IOSimpleLock *lock = _lock;
    if (!lock)
        return;

    bool signal = false;
    IOInterruptState interruptState = IOSimpleLockLockDisableInterrupt(lock);
    if (!_stopping && enqueueLocked(event) && !_pendingSignal) {
        _pendingSignal = 1;
        signal = true;
    }
    IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, interruptState);

    /* IOEventSource::signalWorkAvailable wakes its IOWorkLoop. The event
     * source does the actual callback only in checkForWork() on that loop. */
    if (signal)
        signalWorkAvailable();
}

bool RTW88WCLWorkQueue::checkForWork()
{
    /* Bound one pass so sustained telemetry or state churn cannot monopolize
     * the shared workloop. Returning true asks IOWorkLoop to call us again. */
    uint32_t budget = 32;
    while (budget-- != 0) {
        RTW88WCLRadioEvent event = {};
        RTW88WCLRadioEventHandler *handler = nullptr;
        IOSimpleLock *lock = _lock;
        if (!lock)
            return false;

        IOInterruptState interruptState = IOSimpleLockLockDisableInterrupt(lock);
        if (_stopping || !_events.pop(&event)) {
            if (_stopping)
                _events.clear();
            _pendingSignal = 0;
            IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, interruptState);
            return false;
        }
        handler = _handler;
        IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, interruptState);

        /* WCL controller/interface message translation belongs in this
         * handler, which is guaranteed to execute on the controller workloop. */
        if (handler)
            handler->handleRTW88WCLRadioEvent(event);
    }

    IOSimpleLock *lock = _lock;
    if (!lock)
        return false;
    IOInterruptState interruptState = IOSimpleLockLockDisableInterrupt(lock);
    const bool moreWork = !_stopping && !_events.empty();
    if (!moreWork)
        _pendingSignal = 0;
    IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, interruptState);
    return moreWork;
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLWorkQueue::droppedEvents() const
{
    IOSimpleLock *lock = _lock;
    if (!lock)
        return _dropped;
    IOInterruptState state = IOSimpleLockLockDisableInterrupt(lock);
    const uint32_t value = _dropped;
    IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, state);
    return value;
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLWorkQueue::coalescedRSSIEvents() const
{
    IOSimpleLock *lock = _lock;
    if (!lock)
        return _coalescedRSSI;
    IOInterruptState state = IOSimpleLockLockDisableInterrupt(lock);
    const uint32_t value = _coalescedRSSI;
    IOSimpleLockUnlockEnableInterrupt(lock, state);
    return value;
}

#undef super

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
 * Serialized RTW88 -> native WCL event delivery.
 */
#pragma once

#include <IOKit/IOEventSource.h>
#include <IOKit/IOLocks.h>
#include <IOKit/IOWorkLoop.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.h>

#include "RTW88WCLRadioBridge.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLRadioEventRing.hpp"

class RTW88WCLRadioEventHandler {
public:
    virtual void handleRTW88WCLRadioEvent(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event) = 0;
    virtual ~RTW88WCLRadioEventHandler() {}
};

/* IOEventSource implementation used as the bridge's event sink.
 *
 * Producer context: copy a bounded event record under an interrupt-safe spin
 * lock, then signalWorkAvailable(). No allocation, sleeping, Apple message
 * posting or radio calls occur on this path.
 *
 * Consumer context: IOWorkLoop calls checkForWork(), which drains the ring and
 * invokes the handler on the controller's work-loop thread.
 *
 * Lifecycle contract: detach and quiesce the RTW88 bridge before stop/free.
 * stop() must run outside this event source's active checkForWork()/handler
 * callback; IOWorkLoop removal serializes against any in-flight work check.
 */
class RTW88WCLWorkQueue final : public IOEventSource,
                                public RTW88WCLRadioEventSink {
    OSDeclareDefaultStructors(RTW88WCLWorkQueue)

public:
    using IOEventSource::init;
    static constexpr size_t kCapacity = 128;

    bool init(OSObject *owner, IOWorkLoop *workLoop,
              RTW88WCLRadioEventHandler *handler);
    void stop();
    void free() override;

    void onRTW88WCLRadioEvent(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event) override;
    bool checkForWork() override;

    uint32_t droppedEvents() const;
    uint32_t coalescedRSSIEvents() const;
    bool isInitialized() const { return _lock && _workLoop && _registered; }

private:
    bool enqueueLocked(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event);
    static bool isTelemetryEvent(const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event);

    IOSimpleLock *_lock = nullptr;
    IOWorkLoop *_workLoop = nullptr;               // non-owning
    RTW88WCLRadioEventHandler *_handler = nullptr; // non-owning
    RTW88WCLRadioEventRing<RTW88WCLRadioEvent, kCapacity> _events;
    uint32_t _pendingSignal = 0;   // guarded by _lock
    uint32_t _stopping = 0;        // guarded by _lock
    uint32_t _registered = 0;      // lifecycle-owned
    uint32_t _dropped = 0;         // guarded by _lock
    uint32_t _coalescedRSSI = 0;    // guarded by _lock
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * RTL88WiFi Sequoia 15.8.1 (24H32) WCL interface ABI declarations.
 *
 * This file declares the 24H32 IOSkywalk/IO80211 inheritance and vtable
 * surface needed by the private native WCL frontend. It must only be included
 * by wcl24h32 translation units. Do NOT include legacy IO80211Interface.h,
 * IO80211SkywalkInterface.h or Apple80211.h in the same translation unit.
 *
 * ABI sizes reviewed against the 24H32 x86_64 kernel collection:
 * IOSkywalkInterface 0xb0, IOSkywalkNetworkInterface 0xd0,
 * IOSkywalkEthernetInterface 0x110, IO80211SkywalkInterface 0x118,
 * IO80211InfraInterface/IO80211InfraProtocol 0x120.
 */
#pragma once

#if !defined(KERNEL) || !defined(__cplusplus)
#error "24H32 WCL ABI declarations require kernel C++"
#endif
#ifndef __IO80211_TARGET
#error "Define __IO80211_TARGET for the native WCL target"
#endif
#if __IO80211_TARGET != __MAC_15_2
#error "This ABI header is pinned to macOS 15.2+ / Sequoia Darwin 24"
#endif

/* Native Sequoia link-state values. */
enum IO80211LinkState {
    kIO80211NetworkLinkUndefined = 0,
    kIO80211NetworkLinkDown = 1,
    kIO80211NetworkLinkUp = 2
};
/* The 24H32 WCL header uses this four-byte traffic-class scalar. */
/* apple80211_wme_ac is an opaque four-byte-by-value ABI token. */

class IOSkywalkInterface : public IOService {
    OSDeclareAbstractStructors(IOSkywalkInterface)
public:
    virtual void free() override;
    virtual bool init(OSDictionary *) override;
    virtual bool willTerminate(IOService *, unsigned int) override;
    virtual bool didTerminate(IOService *, unsigned int, bool *) override;
    virtual bool handleOpen(IOService *, unsigned int, void *) override;
    virtual void handleClose(IOService *, unsigned int) override;
    virtual bool handleIsOpen(IOService const *) const override;
    virtual IOReturn enable(unsigned int) = 0;
    virtual IOReturn disable(unsigned int) = 0;
    virtual IOReturn clientConnectWithTask(task *, IOService *, unsigned int);
    virtual void clientDisconnect(IOService *, unsigned int);
    virtual bool isTerminating();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface0();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface1();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface2();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface3();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface4();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface5();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface6();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface7();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface8();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface9();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkInterface10();
private:
    uint8_t _layout[0xb0 - sizeof(IOService)];
};
static_assert(sizeof(IOSkywalkInterface) == 0xb0, "24H32 IOSkywalkInterface ABI");

class IOSkywalkNetworkInterface : public IOSkywalkInterface {
    OSDeclareAbstractStructors(IOSkywalkNetworkInterface)
    /* Private registration payload; its fields are not part of the SDK. */
    struct RegistrationInfo;
public:
    struct IOSkywalkTSOOptions;
    virtual void free() override;
    virtual bool init(OSDictionary *) override;
    virtual void stop(IOService *) override;
    virtual void joinPMtree(IOService *) override;
    virtual IOReturn setAggressiveness(unsigned long, unsigned long) override;
    virtual IOReturn enable(unsigned int) override;
    virtual IOReturn disable(unsigned int) override;
    virtual IOReturn registerNetworkInterfaceWithLogicalLink(RegistrationInfo const *, IOSkywalkLogicalLink *, IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *, IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *, unsigned int);
    /* 24H32 non-virtual registration entry points. Their IOReturn convention
     * is confirmed by the target function bodies and Apple caller's EAX test.
     * These declarations do not add vtable slots. */
    IOReturn registerNetworkInterface(RegistrationInfo const *, IOSkywalkPacketQueue **,
                                      unsigned int, IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *,
                                      IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *, unsigned int);
    IOReturn registerNetworkInterface(RegistrationInfo const *, IOSkywalkLogicalLink *,
                                      IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *,
                                      IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *, unsigned int);
    IOReturn deregisterNetworkInterface(unsigned int);
    virtual IOReturn deregisterLogicalLink();
    virtual SInt32 initBSDInterfaceParameters(ifnet_init_eparams *, sockaddr_dl **) = 0;
    virtual IOReturn prepareBSDInterface(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int);
    virtual void finalizeBSDInterface(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int);
    virtual ifnet_t getBSDInterface() const;
    virtual void setBSDName(char const *);
    virtual const char *getBSDName() const;
    virtual IOReturn processBSDCommand(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int, void *);
    virtual IOReturn processInterfaceCommand(ifdrv *);
    virtual IOReturn interfaceAdvisoryEnable(bool);
    virtual SInt32 setInterfaceEnable(bool);
    virtual SInt32 setRunningState(bool);
    virtual IOReturn handleChosenMedia(unsigned int);
    virtual void *getSupportedMediaArray(unsigned int *, unsigned int *);
    virtual void *getPacketTapInfo(unsigned int *, unsigned int *);
    virtual UInt getUnsentDataByteCount(unsigned int *, unsigned int *, unsigned int) const;
    virtual UInt32 getSupportedWakeFlags(unsigned int *);
    virtual void enableNetworkWake(unsigned int);
    virtual void calculateRingSizeForQueue(IOSkywalkPacketQueue const *, unsigned int *) const;
    virtual UInt getMaxTransferUnit();
    virtual void setMaxTransferUnit(unsigned int);
    virtual UInt getMinPacketSize();
    virtual UInt getHardwareAssists();
    virtual void setHardwareAssists(unsigned int, unsigned int);
    virtual void *getInterfaceFamily();
    virtual void *getInterfaceSubFamily();
    virtual UInt getInitialMedia();
    virtual UInt32 getFeatureFlags();
    virtual UInt getTxDataOffset();
    virtual UInt captureInterfaceState(unsigned int);
    virtual void restoreInterfaceState(unsigned int);
    virtual void setMTU(unsigned int);
    virtual bool bpfTap(unsigned int, unsigned int);
    virtual const char *getBSDNamePrefix();
    virtual UInt getBSDUnitNumber();
    virtual const char *classNameOverride();
    virtual void deferBSDAttach(bool);
    virtual void reportDetailedLinkStatus(if_link_status const *);
    virtual UInt getTSOOptions(IOSkywalkNetworkInterface::IOSkywalkTSOOptions *);
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface0();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface1();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface2();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface3();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface4();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface5();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface6();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface7();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface8();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkNetworkInterface9();
    void reportLinkStatus(unsigned int, unsigned int);
protected:
    /* Kernel-owned expansion allocation: keep its 24H32 extent opaque. */
    struct ExpansionData;
private:
    uint8_t _layout0[0x8];
protected:
    ExpansionData *mExpansionData;
private:
    uint8_t _layout1[0x10];
};
static_assert(sizeof(IOSkywalkNetworkInterface) == 0xd0, "24H32 IOSkywalkNetworkInterface ABI");

class IOSkywalkEthernetInterface : public IOSkywalkNetworkInterface {
    OSDeclareAbstractStructors(IOSkywalkEthernetInterface)
public:
    /* Opaque nested type: public here only so the 24H32 infra shim can spell
     * the exact parameter type. This does not expose or change its layout. */
    struct RegistrationInfo;
    virtual void free() override;
    virtual bool init(OSDictionary *) override;
    virtual IOReturn newUserClient(task *, void *, unsigned int, OSDictionary *, IOUserClient **) override;
    virtual IOReturn setPowerState(unsigned long, IOService *) override;
    virtual IOReturn enable(unsigned int) override;
    virtual SInt32 initBSDInterfaceParameters(ifnet_init_eparams *, sockaddr_dl **) override;
    virtual IOReturn prepareBSDInterface(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int) override;
    virtual IOReturn processBSDCommand(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int, void *) override;
    virtual void *getPacketTapInfo(unsigned int *, unsigned int *) override;
    virtual void enableNetworkWake(unsigned int) override;
    virtual UInt getMaxTransferUnit() override;
    virtual UInt getMinPacketSize() override;
    virtual void *getInterfaceFamily() override;
    virtual void *getInterfaceSubFamily() override;
    virtual UInt getInitialMedia() override;
    virtual const char *getBSDNamePrefix() override;
    virtual IOReturn getHardwareAddress(ether_addr *);
    virtual IOReturn setHardwareAddress(ether_addr *);
    virtual void setLinkLayerAddress(ether_addr *);
    virtual bool configureMulticastFilter(unsigned int, ether_addr const *, unsigned int);
    virtual bool setMulticastAddresses(ether_addr const *, unsigned int);
    virtual void setAllMulticastModeEnable(bool);
    virtual IOReturn setPromiscuousModeEnable(bool, unsigned int);
    virtual void reportNicProxyLimits(nicproxy_limits_info_s);
    virtual void hwConfigNicProxyData(nicproxy_info_s *);
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface0();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface1();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface2();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface3();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface4();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface5();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface6();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface7();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface8();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface9();
    virtual void _RESERVEDIOSkywalkEthernetInterface10();

    /* Non-virtual 24H32 entry points. The complete kernel bodies propagate
     * IOReturn values; Apple callers treat EAX==0 as success. */
    bool initRegistrationInfo(RegistrationInfo *, unsigned int, size_t);
    IOReturn registerEthernetInterface(RegistrationInfo const *, IOSkywalkPacketQueue **,
                                       unsigned int, IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *,
                                       IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *, unsigned int);
    IOReturn registerEthernetInterface(RegistrationInfo const *, IOSkywalkLogicalLink *,
                                       IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *,
                                       IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *, unsigned int);
    IOReturn deregisterEthernetInterface(unsigned int);
protected:
    struct ExpansionData;
private:
    uint8_t _layout0[0x38];
public:
    /* Pointer is visible for layout assertions; its pointed-to layout is not. */
    ExpansionData *mExpansionData2;
};
static_assert(sizeof(IOSkywalkEthernetInterface) == 0x110, "24H32 IOSkywalkEthernetInterface ABI");
static_assert(__offsetof(IOSkywalkEthernetInterface, mExpansionData2) == 0x108, "24H32 Ethernet expansion offset");

struct TxPacketRequest {
    uint16_t unk1, t, mU, mM, pkt_cnt, unk2, unk3, unk4;
    uint32_t pad;
    mbuf_t bufs[8];
    uint32_t reqTx;
};
static_assert(sizeof(TxPacketRequest) == 0x60, "24H32 TxPacketRequest ABI");

class IO80211SkywalkInterface : public IOSkywalkEthernetInterface {
    OSDeclareAbstractStructors(IO80211SkywalkInterface)
public:
    virtual bool init() override;
    virtual void free() override;
    virtual IOReturn configureReport(IOReportChannelList *, unsigned int, void *, void *) override;
    virtual IOReturn updateReport(IOReportChannelList *, unsigned int, void *, void *) override;
    virtual bool start(IOService *) override;
    virtual void stop(IOService *) override;
    virtual IOReturn newUserClient(task *, void *, unsigned int, OSDictionary *, IOUserClient **) override;
    virtual const char *stringFromReturn(int) override;
    virtual int errnoFromReturn(int) override;
    virtual IOReturn setPowerState(unsigned long, IOService *) override;
    virtual unsigned long maxCapabilityForDomainState(unsigned long) override;
    virtual unsigned long initialPowerStateForDomainState(unsigned long) override;
    virtual IOReturn enable(unsigned int) override;
    virtual IOReturn disable(unsigned int) override;
    virtual SInt32 initBSDInterfaceParameters(ifnet_init_eparams *, sockaddr_dl **) override;
    virtual IOReturn prepareBSDInterface(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int) override;
    virtual IOReturn processBSDCommand(struct __ifnet *, unsigned int, void *) override;
    virtual SInt32 setInterfaceEnable(bool) override;
    virtual SInt32 setRunningState(bool) override;
    virtual IOReturn handleChosenMedia(unsigned int) override;
    virtual void *getSupportedMediaArray(unsigned int *, unsigned int *) override;
    virtual UInt32 getFeatureFlags() override;
    virtual const char *classNameOverride() override;
    virtual IOReturn getHardwareAddress(ether_addr *) override;
    virtual IOReturn setHardwareAddress(ether_addr *) override;
    virtual IOReturn setPromiscuousModeEnable(bool, unsigned int) override;
    virtual void *createPeerManager();
private:
    virtual void _abi_0b10() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface10createPeerEPKhP18IO80211PeerManager");
public:
    virtual void postMessage(unsigned int, void *, unsigned long, bool);
    virtual IOReturn reportDataPathEvents(unsigned int, void *, unsigned long, bool);
    virtual IOReturn recordOutputPackets(TxSubmissionDequeueStats *, TxSubmissionDequeueStats *);
    virtual IOReturn recordOutputPacket(apple80211_wme_ac, int, int);
    virtual void logTxPacket(IO80211NetworkPacket *, PacketSkywalkScratch *, apple80211_wme_ac, bool);
    virtual void logTxCompletionPacket(IO80211NetworkPacket *, PacketSkywalkScratch *, unsigned char *, apple80211_wme_ac, int, unsigned int, bool, bool);
    virtual IOReturn recordCompletionPackets(TxCompletionEnqueueStats *, TxCompletionEnqueueStats *);
    virtual IOReturn inputPacket(IO80211NetworkPacket *, packet_info_tag *, ether_header *, bool *, bool);
    virtual IOReturn forwardInfraRelayPackets(IO80211NetworkPacket *, ether_header *);
    virtual void logSkywalkTxReqPacket(IO80211NetworkPacket *, PacketSkywalkScratch *, unsigned char *, apple80211_wme_ac, bool);
    virtual SInt64 pendingPackets(unsigned char);
    virtual SInt64 packetSpace(unsigned char);
    virtual bool isChipInterfaceReady();
    virtual bool isDebounceOnGoing();
    virtual bool setLinkState(IO80211LinkState, unsigned int, bool, unsigned int);
    virtual IO80211LinkState linkState();
    virtual void setScanningState(unsigned int, bool, apple80211_scan_data *, int);
    virtual void setDataPathState(bool);
    virtual void *getScanManager();
    virtual IO80211Controller *getController();
    virtual void updateLinkParameters(apple80211_interface_availability *);
    virtual void updateInterfaceCoexRiskPct(unsigned long long);
    virtual void setLQM(unsigned long long);
    virtual void updateLinkStatus();
    virtual void updateLinkStatusGated();
    virtual void setInterfaceExtendedCCA(apple80211_channel, apple80211_cca_report *);
    virtual void setInterfaceCCA(apple80211_channel, int);
    virtual void setInterfaceNF(apple80211_channel, long long);
    virtual void setInterfaceOFDMDesense(apple80211_channel, long long);
private:
    virtual void _abi_0bf8() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface17removePacketQueueEP20IO80211FlowQueueHash");
public:
    virtual void setDebugFlags(unsigned long long, unsigned int);
    virtual SInt64 debugFlags();
    virtual void setInterfaceChipCounters(apple80211_stat_report *, apple80211_chip_counters_tx *, apple80211_chip_error_counters_tx *, apple80211_chip_counters_rx *);
    virtual void setInterfaceMIBdot11(apple80211_stat_report *, apple80211_ManagementInformationBasedot11_counters *);
    virtual void setFrameStats(apple80211_stat_report *, apple80211_frame_counters *);
    virtual void setInfraSpecificFrameStats(apple80211_stat_report *, apple80211_infra_specific_stats *);
    virtual SInt64 getWmeTxCounters(unsigned long long *);
    virtual void setPeerManagerLogFlag(unsigned int, unsigned int, unsigned int);
    virtual void setWoWEnabled(bool);
    virtual bool wowEnabled();
    virtual void printDataPath(userPrintCtx *);
    virtual UInt32 getDataQueueDepth();
private:
    virtual void _abi_0c68() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface21findOrCreateFlowQueueE20IO80211FlowQueueHash");
    virtual void _abi_0c70() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface30findOrCreateFlowQueueWithCacheE20IO80211FlowQueueHashPb");
    virtual void _abi_0c78() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface21findExistingFlowQueueE20IO80211FlowQueueHash");
    virtual void _abi_0c80() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface17removePacketQueueEPK20IO80211FlowQueueHash");
public:
    virtual void flushPacketQueues();
    virtual void cachePeer(ether_addr *, unsigned int *);
    virtual bool shouldLog(unsigned long long);
    virtual void vlogDebug(unsigned long long, char const *, va_list);
    virtual void vlogDebugBPF(unsigned long long, char const *, va_list);
    virtual UInt64 createLinkQualityMonitor(IO80211Peer *, IOService *);
    virtual void releaseLinkQualityMonitor(IO80211Peer *);
    virtual void *getP2PSkywalkPeerMgr();
    virtual bool isCommandProhibited(int);
private:
    virtual void _abi_0cd0() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface8findPeerER10ether_addr");
public:
    virtual void setNotificationProperty(OSSymbol const *, OSObject const *);
    virtual void *getWorkerMatchingDict(OSString *);
    virtual bool init(IOService *, ether_addr *);
    virtual bool isInterfaceEnabled();
    virtual ether_addr *getSelfMacAddr();
    virtual IOReturn setMacAddress(ether_addr &) = 0;
    virtual void *getPacketPool(OSString *);
    virtual CCLogStream *getLogger() const;

    virtual void destroyEventPipe(IO80211APIUserClient *);

    virtual void _abi_0dd0() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface17setUserBufferInfoEP18IOMemoryDescriptory");
public:
    virtual void postMessageIOUC(char const *, unsigned int, void *, unsigned long);
    virtual bool isIOUCPipeOpened();
    virtual void *getRingMD(IO80211APIUserClient *, unsigned long long);
private:
    virtual void _abi_0df0() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface10attachPeerEP10ether_addr");
    virtual void _abi_0df8() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface10detachPeerEP10ether_addr");
    virtual void _abi_0e00() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface21setDebugTrafficReportEb");
    virtual void _abi_0e08() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface25getDataPathInterfaceStatsEP36apple80211_data_path_interface_stats");
    virtual void _abi_0e10() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface20getDataPathPeerStatsEP31apple80211_data_path_peer_stats");
    virtual void _abi_0e18() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface22getLastQueuePacketTimeEP10ether_addr");
    virtual void _abi_0e20() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface32getLastRxUnicastLinkActivityTimeEP10ether_addr");
    virtual void _abi_0e28() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface24updateInterfaceDataStatsEP36apple80211_data_path_interface_stats");
    virtual void _abi_0e30() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface19updatePeerDataStatsEP31apple80211_data_path_peer_stats");
    virtual void _abi_0e38() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface12logTxLatencyEPhjy");
    virtual void _abi_0e40() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface12logRxLatencyEjy");

    virtual void _abi_0e58() __asm__("__ZN23IO80211SkywalkInterface18getLastRxTimeStampERy");

    uint8_t _layout[0x118 - sizeof(IOSkywalkEthernetInterface)];
public:
    OSString *setInterfaceRole(unsigned int);
    void *setInterfaceId(unsigned int);
    int getInterfaceRole();
};

    RTW88_WCL_ABI_METHOD(setMWS_ACCESSORY_POWER_LIMIT_WIFI_ENH, apple80211_mws_accessory_power_limit)

};
static_assert(sizeof(IO80211InfraProtocol) == 0x120, "24H32 IO80211InfraProtocol ABI");

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Sequoia 24H32 native IO80211Controller with staged backend ownership.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLController.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCL24H32InterfaceABI.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter.hpp"
#include "../RTW88IEEE80211.hpp"

#include <IOKit/pci/IOPCIDevice.h>

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <IOKit/network/IOOutputQueue.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.h>

#define super IO80211Controller
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLController, IO80211Controller)

bool RTW88WCLController::init(OSDictionary *properties)
{
    if (!super::init(properties))
        return false;
    _workLoop = nullptr;
    _packetPathInterface = nullptr;
    _packetPathState = kPacketPathDisabled;
    _packetPathInFlight = 0;
    _pciBackend = nullptr;
    _skywalkQueues = nullptr;
    _rxAdapter = nullptr;
    _stagedRxPackets.reset();
    _radio = nullptr;
    _baseStarted = false;
    _radioStarted = false;
    return true;
}

bool RTW88WCLController::createWorkLoop()
{
    if (_workLoop)
        return true;
    _workLoop = IOWorkLoop::workLoop();
    return _workLoop != nullptr;
}

IOWorkLoop *RTW88WCLController::getWorkLoop() const
{
    return _workLoop;
}

bool RTW88WCLController::start(IOService *provider)
{
    if (!provider || _baseStarted || _pciBackend || _radio)
        return false;
    if (!super::start(provider))
        return false;
    _baseStarted = true;

    /* Stage A: the controller workloop must exist before acquiring a PCI
     * backend or registering any interrupt source. */
    if (!createWorkLoop()) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: controller workloop creation failed\n");
        goto fail;
    }

    /* Stage B: this native personality must be matched directly to a PCI
     * provider. The backend retains it and owns BAR2/MMIO/DMA resources. */
    {
        IOPCIDevice *pci = OSDynamicCast(IOPCIDevice, provider);
        if (!pci) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: provider is not an IOPCIDevice\n");
            goto fail;
        }

        _pciBackend = new RTW88WCLPCIBackend;
        if (!_pciBackend) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI backend allocation failed\n");
            goto fail;
        }
        if (!_pciBackend->init()) {
            _pciBackend->release();
            _pciBackend = nullptr;
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI backend initialization failed\n");
            goto fail;
        }

        const IOReturn openStatus = _pciBackend->open(pci, _workLoop, this);
        if (openStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI backend open failed: 0x%08x\n",
                  openStatus);
            goto fail;
        }

        /* Stage C: instantiate the RTW88 state machine against the backend's
         * compat pci_dev. This installs callback context and allocates its
         * private timers/workloop only; it does not probe or power the chip. */
        struct pci_dev *compatPCI = _pciBackend->compatPCIDevice();
        if (!compatPCI) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: backend did not provide a compat PCI device\n");
            goto fail;
        }
        RTW88IEEE80211 *radio = RTW88IEEE80211::createAirport(this, compatPCI);
        if (!radio) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: RTW88 state-machine allocation failed\n");
            goto fail;
        }
        radio->setEventDelegate(this);
        const IOReturn bindStatus = bindRadioBackend(radio);
        radio->release();
        if (bindStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: RTW88 state-machine bind failed: 0x%08x\n",
                  bindStatus);
            goto fail;
        }
    }

    /* Stage D: reserve native Skywalk queue/pool objects while the PCI and
     * RTW88 owners are still available for orderly unwind. These objects are
     * intentionally not registered or scheduled until packet descriptor
     * conversion and hardware completion correlation are implemented. */
    _skywalkQueues = new RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues;
    if (!_skywalkQueues || !_skywalkQueues->init()) {
        if (_skywalkQueues) {
            _skywalkQueues->release();
            _skywalkQueues = nullptr;
        }
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: Skywalk queue owner allocation failed\n");
        goto fail;
    }
    {
        const IOReturn queueStatus = _skywalkQueues->createQueues();
        if (queueStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: Skywalk queue/pool allocation failed: 0x%08x\n",
                  queueStatus);
            goto fail;
        }
    }

    /* Stage E: bind the RX workloop consumer to the now-owned 4 KiB RX pool.
     * It copies frames into native packet buffers, but its sink remains
     * rejecting until the registration/completion ABI is closed. */
    _rxAdapter = new RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter;
    if (!_rxAdapter || !_rxAdapter->init(
            this, _workLoop, _skywalkQueues->rxPacketPool(),
            &RTW88WCLController::stagedRxFrame, this)) {
        if (_rxAdapter) {
            _rxAdapter->release();
            _rxAdapter = nullptr;
        }
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: RX packet adapter initialization failed\n");
        goto fail;
    }

    provider->setProperty("RTW88WCLControllerStage",
                           "PCI, RTW88 and pool-backed RX packet conversion staged; datapath pending");

    /* Publication fence: do not probe or power the radio until Skywalk packet
     * registration, RX/TX rings, packet metadata and teardown are implemented.
     * Starting it sooner would produce frames without a safe delivery path. */
    IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: backend lifecycle staged; refusing publication until Skywalk datapath is ready\n");
    goto fail;

fail:
    {
        const IOReturn cleanupStatus = releaseNativeResources();
        if (cleanupStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: start-failure cleanup incomplete (0x%08x); retaining native resources\n",
                  cleanupStatus);
            return false;
        }
    }
    if (_baseStarted) {
        super::stop(provider);
        _baseStarted = false;
    }
    return false;
}

void RTW88WCLController::stop(IOService *provider)
{
    const IOReturn cleanupStatus = releaseNativeResources();
    if (cleanupStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: stop cleanup incomplete (0x%08x); deferring superclass stop and retaining resources\n",
              cleanupStatus);
        return;
    }
    if (_baseStarted) {
        super::stop(provider);
        _baseStarted = false;
    }
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLController::releaseNativeResources()
{
    const IOReturn packetPathStatus = quiescePacketPath();
    if (packetPathStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: packet path quiesce deferred: 0x%08x\n",
              packetPathStatus);
        return packetPathStatus; // Keep the radio, interface and PCI callbacks alive.
    }

    /* Stop the RTW88 RX/TX producer before removing its deferred consumers or
     * disabling PCI interrupts. The staged path currently leaves
     * _radioStarted false; preserve this ordering when native radio start is
     * enabled after queue registration. */
    if (_radio && _radioStarted) {
        _radio->stop();
        _radioStarted = false;
    }

    if (_pciBackend && _pciBackend->isRadioRunning()) {
        const IOReturn fenceStatus = _pciBackend->setRadioRunning(false);
        if (fenceStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI interrupt/bus-master fence failed: 0x%08x\n",
                  fenceStatus);
            return fenceStatus; // Keep callbacks, mappings and DMA owner alive on failure.
        }
    }

    /* Remove the RX workloop source only after the RTW88 producer is stopped.
     * stop() waits out an active checkForWork() and releases all staged mbufs. */
    if (_rxAdapter) {
        _rxAdapter->stop();
        _rxAdapter->release();
        _rxAdapter = nullptr;
    }

    /* The RX adapter transfers accepted native packets into the controller's
     * bounded ready ring. Recycle those packets while their pool still exists;
     * on a pool error, retain the queue owner and PCI/backend context so a later
     * stop/free attempt can retry rather than releasing live packet memory. */
    const IOReturn recycleStatus = recycleStagedRxPackets();
    if (recycleStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: staged RX packet recycle deferred: 0x%08x\n",
              recycleStatus);
        return recycleStatus;
    }

    /* Skywalk packet pools/queues are released only after both the mbuf staging
     * adapter and the prepared-packet ownership ring have been drained. */
    if (_skywalkQueues) {
        _skywalkQueues->destroyQueues();
        _skywalkQueues->release();
        _skywalkQueues = nullptr;
    }

    

    /* RTW88IEEE80211 owns the compat callback context; destroy it before
     * rtw88_compat_exit() and before relinquishing the global hardware lease. */
    unbindRadioBackend();

    if (_pciBackend) {
        const IOReturn closeStatus = _pciBackend->close();
        if (closeStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI backend close deferred: 0x%08x\n",
                  closeStatus);
            return closeStatus;
        }
        _pciBackend->release();
        _pciBackend = nullptr;
    }
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

void RTW88WCLController::free()
{
    const IOReturn cleanupStatus = releaseNativeResources();
    if (cleanupStatus != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: controller shutdown incomplete (0x%08x); quarantining controller and retaining workloop/resources\n",
              cleanupStatus);
        /* Do not release the workloop or call super::free() while any child,
         * packet pool, or PCI/DMA resource may still reference this controller.
         * This deliberately leaks the object rather than risking a UAF/DMA UAF. */
        return;
    }
    if (_workLoop) {
        _workLoop->release();
        _workLoop = nullptr;
    }
    super::free();
}

{
    (void)command;
    return true; // No WCL interface is published by this ABI-only slice.
}

bool RTW88WCLController::enterPacketPath()
{
    if (__atomic_load_n(&_packetPathState, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) !=
        kPacketPathActive)
        return false;

    __atomic_add_fetch(&_packetPathInFlight, 1U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
    if (__atomic_load_n(&_packetPathState, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) ==
        kPacketPathActive)
        return true;

    __atomic_sub_fetch(&_packetPathInFlight, 1U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
    return false;
}

void RTW88WCLController::leavePacketPath()
{
    __atomic_sub_fetch(&_packetPathInFlight, 1U, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLController::activatePacketPath(
    IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface)
{
    if (!interface)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_radio || !_radioStarted || !_pciBackend || !_pciBackend->isOpen())
        return kIOReturnNotReady;

    uint32_t expected = kPacketPathDisabled;
    if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_packetPathState, &expected,
                                     kPacketPathBinding, false,
                                     __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    interface->retain();
    _packetPathInterface = interface;
    __atomic_store_n(&_packetPathState, kPacketPathActive, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: native mbuf packet handoff enabled\n");
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLController::quiescePacketPath()
{
    for (;;) {
        uint32_t state = __atomic_load_n(&_packetPathState, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
        if (state == kPacketPathDisabled)
            return kIOReturnSuccess;
        if (state == kPacketPathBinding)
            return kIOReturnBusy;
        if (state == kPacketPathActive) {
            uint32_t expected = kPacketPathActive;
            if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_packetPathState, &expected,
                                             kPacketPathQuiescing, false,
                                             __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
                continue;
        } else if (state != kPacketPathQuiescing) {
            return kIOReturnBusy;
        }
        break;
    }

    if (__atomic_load_n(&_packetPathInFlight, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0)
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    uint32_t expected = kPacketPathQuiescing;
    if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_packetPathState, &expected,
                                     kPacketPathReleasing, false,
                                     __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface = _packetPathInterface;
    _packetPathInterface = nullptr;
    if (interface)
        interface->release();
    __atomic_store_n(&_packetPathState, kPacketPathDisabled, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

UInt32 RTW88WCLController::outputPacket(mbuf_t packet, void *param)
{
    (void)param;
    if (!packet)
        return kIOReturnOutputDropped;

    if (!enterPacketPath()) {
        mbuf_freem(packet);
        return kIOReturnOutputDropped;
    }

    /* RTW88IEEE80211::outputPacket consumes the mbuf on every return path. */
    const UInt32 result = _radio ? _radio->outputPacket(packet)
                                 : kIOReturnOutputDropped;
    leavePacketPath();
    return result;
}

void RTW88WCLController::rtw88WCLResumeTransmit()
{
    /* RTW88's internal TX queues resume independently. The future Skywalk
     * submission queue will connect its wakeup here after ring registration. */
}

void RTW88WCLController::rtw88Event(RTW88Event event, void *data)
{
    (void)event;
    (void)data;
    /* WCL notifications are withheld until interface registration succeeds. */
}

mbuf_t RTW88WCLController::allocateInputPacket(uint32_t length)
{
    if (length < 14 || length > RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::kMaximumFrameLength ||
        !_radio || !_rxAdapter || !enterPacketPath())
        return nullptr;

    /* IO80211Controller inherits the IONetworkController packet allocator.
     * Keep its mbuf metadata initialization rather than hand-rolling m_len. */
    mbuf_t packet = allocatePacket(length);
    leavePacketPath();
    return packet;
}

void RTW88WCLController::injectRxFrame(mbuf_t packet)
{
    if (!packet)
        return;

    if (!_rxAdapter || !enterPacketPath()) {
        mbuf_freem(packet);
        return;
    }

    /* enqueueFrame consumes the mbuf on both success and failure. The native
     * RX completion queue is not registered yet, so the workloop sink below
     * deliberately drops frames after staging rather than exposing them. */
    (void)_rxAdapter->enqueueFrame(packet);
    leavePacketPath();
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLController::recycleStagedRxPackets()
{
    if (_stagedRxPackets.empty())
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    if (!_skywalkQueues || !_skywalkQueues->rxPacketPool())
        return kIOReturnNotReady;

    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *pool = _skywalkQueues->rxPacketPool();
    IOSkywalkPacket *packet = nullptr;
    bool disposalRequired = false;
    while (_stagedRxPackets.pop(&packet)) {
        if (!packet)
            continue;

        const IOReturn status = pool->deallocatePacket(packet);
        if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
            /* The radio and RX event source are already quiesced, and these
             * queues were never registered. If a packet cannot be returned
             * individually, dispose the pool's packet set as one teardown
             * operation before its memory segments are released. */
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: RX packet recycle returned 0x%08x; pool disposal required\n",
                  status);
            disposalRequired = true;
        }
        packet = nullptr;
    }

    if (disposalRequired)
        pool->disposeAllPackets();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

bool RTW88WCLController::stagedRxFrame(
    IOSkywalkPacket *preparedPacket, void *context)
{
    auto *self = static_cast<RTW88WCLController *>(context);
    if (!self || !preparedPacket)
        return false;

    /* Preserve the pool-backed packet in a fixed-capacity owner ring. The
     * ring is bounded to the RX pool's 128 packets; overflow returns false,
     * causing the RX adapter to recycle the packet immediately. No queue API
     * is called here: completion-queue registration/ownership is still fenced. */
    return self->_stagedRxPackets.push(preparedPacket);
}

IOWorkLoop *RTW88WCLController::getRxWorkLoop()
{
    return _workLoop;
}

void RTW88WCLController::setLinkStatus(UInt32 status)
{
    (void)status;
    /* Link state is owned by IO80211InfraProtocol after Skywalk attachment. */
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLController::bindRadioBackend(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio)
{
    if (!radio) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (_radio) return kIOReturnBusy;
    radio->retain();
    _radio = radio;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

void RTW88WCLController::unbindRadioBackend()
{
    if (_radio) {
        _radio->release();
        _radio = nullptr;
    }
}
