/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * First native WCL controller slice for Sequoia 24H32.
 *
 * This controller remains isolated from the active legacy Kext. It owns the
 * native PCI backend and RTW88 state-machine object, but deliberately does
 * not start the radio or publish an interface until the Skywalk datapath is
 * complete.
 */
#pragma once

#include "RTW88WCL24H32ABI.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLPCIBackend.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLMbufRing.hpp"
#include <IOKit/IOWorkLoop.h>

class RTW88IEEE80211;
class RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues;
class RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter;
class IOSkywalkPacket;

class RTW88WCLController final : public IO80211Controller,
                                 public RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient,
                                 public RTW88EventDelegate,
                                 public RTW88RxDelegate {
    OSDeclareDefaultStructors(RTW88WCLController)

public:
    bool init(OSDictionary *properties) override;
    bool start(IOService *provider) override;
    void stop(IOService *provider) override;
    void free() override;
    bool createWorkLoop() override;
    IOWorkLoop *getWorkLoop() const override;

    /* Native Ethernet mbuf handoff seam. Skywalk queue callbacks must call these
     * only after their packet rings are registered and the radio is started. */
    UInt32 outputPacket(mbuf_t packet, void *param) override;
    IOReturn activatePacketPath(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface);
    IOReturn quiescePacketPath();

    /* Required pure virtuals in the 24H32 IO80211Controller ABI. Until the
     * native protocol + Skywalk datapath are attached, operations fail closed. */
    bool isCommandProhibited(int command) override;
    SInt32 handleCardSpecific(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                              unsigned long command, void *data,
                              bool set) override;
    IOReturn getDRIVER_VERSION(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                               apple80211_version_data *data) override;
    IOReturn getHARDWARE_VERSION(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                                 apple80211_version_data *data) override;
    IOReturn getCARD_CAPABILITIES(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                                  apple80211_capability_data *data) override;
    IOReturn getPOWER(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                      apple80211_power_data *data) override;
    IOReturn setPOWER(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                      apple80211_power_data *data) override;
    IOReturn getCOUNTRY_CODE(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                             apple80211_country_code_data *data) override;
    IOReturn setCOUNTRY_CODE(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                             apple80211_country_code_data *data) override;
    IOReturn setGET_DEBUG_INFO(IO80211SkywalkInterface *interface,
                               apple80211_debug_command *data) override;
    CCLogStream *getLogger() const override;
    CommonFaultReporter *getFaultReporterFromDriver() override;

    IOReturn bindRadioBackend(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio);
    void unbindRadioBackend();
    RTW88IEEE80211 *radioBackend() const { return _radio; }
    RTW88WCLPCIBackend *pciBackend() const { return _pciBackend; }

    /* RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient: no native TX queue is registered yet. */
    void rtw88WCLResumeTransmit() override;

    /* RTW88EventDelegate: events are deliberately not published until the
     * Skywalk interface has completed registration. */
    void rtw88Event(RTW88Event event, void *data) override;

    /* RTW88RxDelegate: receive mbufs enter a bounded workloop staging ring,
     * then become pool-backed Skywalk packets. The sink still rejects them
     * until the RX completion queue is registered and verified. */
    mbuf_t allocateInputPacket(uint32_t length) override;
    void injectRxFrame(mbuf_t packet) override;
    IOWorkLoop *getRxWorkLoop() override;
    void setLinkStatus(UInt32 status) override;

private:
    IOReturn releaseNativeResources();
    IOReturn recycleStagedRxPackets();
    static bool stagedRxFrame(IOSkywalkPacket *preparedPacket, void *context);

    enum : uint32_t {
        kPacketPathDisabled = 0,
        kPacketPathActive = 1,
        kPacketPathQuiescing = 2,
        kPacketPathReleasing = 3,
        kPacketPathBinding = 4
    };

    bool enterPacketPath();
    void leavePacketPath();

    static constexpr uint32_t kStagedRxPacketCapacity = 128;

    IOWorkLoop *_workLoop = nullptr;
    IO80211SkywalkInterface *_packetPathInterface = nullptr; // retained while active
    volatile uint32_t _packetPathState = kPacketPathDisabled;
    volatile uint32_t _packetPathInFlight = 0;
    RTW88WCLPCIBackend *_pciBackend = nullptr; // retained
    RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues *_skywalkQueues = nullptr; // retained, unregistered
    RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter *_rxAdapter = nullptr; // retained, workloop-bound
    /* Accepted RX packets remain pool-owned here until a verified completion
     * queue can consume them. This ring is bounded to the RX pool packet count. */
    RTW88WCLMbufRing<IOSkywalkPacket *, kStagedRxPacketCapacity> _stagedRxPackets;
    RTW88IEEE80211 *_radio = nullptr;          // retained via bindRadioBackend
    bool _baseStarted = false;
    bool _radioStarted = false;
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * RTL88WiFi native Sequoia 24H32 IO80211InfraProtocol implementation slice.
 *
 * This translation unit is compile-only for now. It must remain separate from
 * the legacy Airport/IO80211FamilyLegacy translation units until the native
 * controller owns PCI/MMIO/DMA and the Skywalk datapath is integrated.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLController.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLInfraInterface.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.h>
#include <string.h>

#define super IO80211InfraProtocol
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLInfraInterface, IO80211InfraProtocol)

namespace {
enum : unsigned int {
    kWCLMessageDeauth = 203,
    kWCLMessageAssocComplete = 211,
    kWCLMessageConnectComplete = 213,
    kWCLMessageJoinAbortComplete = 214,
    kWCLMessageLinkStatus = 216,
    kWCLMessageLqmUpdate = 39,
    kWCLMessageScanResult = 201,
    kWCLMessageScanDone = 237
};

struct RTW88WCLBeaconMetadata {
    uint32_t ieLength;
    uint16_t channelSpec;
    uint8_t ssid[32];
    uint8_t ssidLength;
    uint8_t channel;
    uint8_t reserved0;
    uint8_t bssid[6];
    uint8_t reserved1;
    int32_t rssi;
    int16_t noise;
    int16_t snr;
    uint16_t interval;
    uint16_t capability;
    uint32_t flags;
};
static_assert(sizeof(RTW88WCLBeaconMetadata) == 64,
              "Sequoia WCL beacon message header size");

enum : uint32_t {
    kWCLBeaconSSIDPresent = 1U << 1,
    kWCLBeaconRSSIValid = 1U << 14
};

struct RTW88WCLAssociationEvent {
    uint16_t status;
    uint16_t reason;
    uint8_t mlo;
    uint8_t bssid[6];
    uint8_t reserved;
    uint32_t mloData[2];
    int32_t driverStatus;
    int32_t driverReason;
};
static_assert(sizeof(RTW88WCLAssociationEvent) == 28,
              "Sequoia WCL association event size");

struct RTW88WCLConnectionPeer {
    uint8_t bssid[6];
    uint16_t reserved;
    int32_t driverStatus;
    int32_t driverReason;
};
static_assert(sizeof(RTW88WCLConnectionPeer) == 16,
              "Sequoia WCL connection peer size");

struct RTW88WCLConnectionEvent {
    uint16_t status;
    uint16_t reason;
    RTW88WCLConnectionPeer peers[10];
};
static_assert(sizeof(RTW88WCLConnectionEvent) == 164,
              "Sequoia WCL connection event size");

struct RTW88WCLLinkStatusEvent {
    uint8_t bssid[6];
    uint8_t up;
    uint8_t reserved;
    int32_t reason;
};
static_assert(sizeof(RTW88WCLLinkStatusEvent) == 12,
              "Sequoia WCL link status event size");

struct RTW88WCLLqmUpdate {
    uint8_t rssiValid;
    uint8_t reserved[3];
    int32_t rssi;
    uint8_t tail[0x150 - 8];
};
static_assert(sizeof(RTW88WCLLqmUpdate) == 0x150,
              "Sequoia WCL LQM update size");

static bool isZeroAddress(const uint8_t *address, size_t length)
{
    if (!address) return true;
    uint8_t aggregate = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) aggregate |= address[i];
    return aggregate == 0;
}

static bool validInformationElements(const uint8_t *ies, size_t length)
{
    if (length && !ies) return false;
    size_t offset = 0;
    while (offset < length) {
        if (length - offset < 2) return false;
        const size_t elementLength = ies[offset + 1];
        if (elementLength > length - offset - 2) return false;
        offset += 2 + elementLength;
    }
    return true;
}

/* WCL's 24H32 setCIPHER_KEY path carries PMK/PMKSA/MSK semantics. It is not
 * the legacy PTK/GTK key slot. The current RTW88 WCL path supplies the PMK in
 * ASSOCIATE and runs the internal WPA2 handshake; enterprise PMKSA insertion
 * is not implemented here, so setCIPHER_KEY must fail closed. */

static uint16_t channelFlags(const RTW88Channel &channel)
{
    uint32_t flags = APPLE80211_C_FLAG_20MHZ;
    flags |= channel.number <= 14 ? APPLE80211_C_FLAG_2GHZ
                                  : APPLE80211_C_FLAG_5GHZ;
    if (channel.radar) flags |= APPLE80211_C_FLAG_DFS;
    if (!channel.passive && !channel.radar) flags |= APPLE80211_C_FLAG_ACTIVE;
    return static_cast<uint16_t>(flags);
}
} // namespace

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::init()
{
    if (!super::init())
        return false;
    clearRegistrationInfo();
    _controller = nullptr;
    _workQueue = nullptr;
    _scanFilter = {};
    bzero(_targetBSSID, sizeof(_targetBSSID));
    _scanActive = false;
    _activeScanGeneration = 0;
    _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
    _assocNotified = false;
    _connectNotified = false;
    _stopping = false;
    return true;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::init(IOService *provider, ether_addr *address)
{
    if (!provider || !address || !super::init())
        return false;

    _controller = OSDynamicCast(RTW88WCLController, provider);
    if (!_controller)
        return false;

    if (!IO80211SkywalkInterface::init(provider, address))
        return false;

    clearRegistrationInfo();
    return true;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::attachRadio(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio)
{
    if (!radio || !_controller)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (_bridge.isAttached() || _workQueue)
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    IOWorkLoop *workLoop = _controller->getWorkLoop();
    if (!workLoop)
        return kIOReturnNotReady;

    RTW88WCLWorkQueue *queue = new RTW88WCLWorkQueue;
    if (!queue)
        return kIOReturnNoMemory;
    if (!queue->init(this, workLoop, this)) {
        queue->release();
        return kIOReturnNoResources;
    }

    IOReturn status = _bridge.attach(radio, queue);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        queue->stop();
        queue->release();
        return status;
    }
    _workQueue = queue;
    _stopping = false;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::detachRadio()
{
    _stopping = true;
    /* Quiesce the producer first, then remove the work source. The controller
     * must stop the radio before destroying its backend object. */
    _bridge.detach();
    if (_workQueue) {
        _workQueue->stop();
        _workQueue->release();
        _workQueue = nullptr;
    }
    _scanActive = false;
    _activeScanGeneration = 0;
    _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
    _assocNotified = false;
    _connectNotified = false;
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::free()
{
    clearRegistrationInfo();
    detachRadio();
    _controller = nullptr;
    super::free();
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::clearRegistrationInfo()
{
    RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32::clear(_registrationInfo);
    _registrationInfoPrepared = false;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::prepareRegistrationInfo(const ether_addr &address)
{
    using namespace RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32;

    clearRegistrationInfo();
    auto *info = reinterpret_cast<IOSkywalkEthernetInterface::RegistrationInfo *>(
        _registrationInfo.bytes);

    /* 24H32 initRegistrationInfo requires option 1 and the exact 0x130 size.
     * It supplies the other opaque slots through this interface's virtual
     * hooks; do not replace it with a guessed local structure initializer.
     */
    if (!initRegistrationInfo(info, 1, sizeof(_registrationInfo.bytes))) {
        clearRegistrationInfo();
        return false;
    }

    if (!applyAppleProducerWrites(_registrationInfo, address.octet) ||
        !matchesApple24H32Template(_registrationInfo, address.octet)) {
        clearRegistrationInfo();
        return false;
    }

    _registrationInfoPrepared = true;
    return true;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::start(IOService *provider)
{
    /* The native controller has not yet completed PCI/DMA ownership transfer,
     * Skywalk registration-info allocation or RX/TX ring registration. Never
     * publish a half-initialized Wi-Fi service. */
    if (provider) {
        provider->setProperty("RTW88WCLInfraInterfaceStage",
                              "24H32 protocol handlers; Skywalk datapath pending");
    }
    return false;
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::stop(IOService *provider)
{
    clearRegistrationInfo();
    detachRadio();
    if (provider)
        provider->setProperty("RTW88WCLInfraInterfaceStopped", kOSBooleanTrue);
    super::stop(provider);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::enable(unsigned int options)
{
    (void)options;
    if (_stopping || !_bridge.isAttached() || !_controller)
        return kIOReturnNotReady;

    /* Bring the RTW88 station path up first. Only then may the controller's
     * standard mbuf TX hook admit packets for this registered interface. */
    IOReturn status = _bridge.powerOn();
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess)
        return status;

    status = _controller->activatePacketPath(this);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        _bridge.powerOff();
        return status;
    }
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::disable(unsigned int options)
{
    (void)options;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached() || !_controller)
        return kIOReturnNotReady;

    /* Stop new mbuf submissions and wait for synchronous TX handoffs before
     * asking RTW88 to power down. A busy path leaves the radio running. */
    IOReturn status = _controller->quiescePacketPath();
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess)
        return status;
    return _bridge.powerOff();
}

SInt32 RTW88WCLInfraInterface::initBSDInterfaceParameters(
    ifnet_init_eparams *params, sockaddr_dl **address)
{
    (void)params;
    (void)address;
    return kIOReturnUnsupported;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setMacAddress(ether_addr &address)
{
    return _bridge.setMACAddress(address.octet);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getHardwareAddress(ether_addr *address)
{
    if (!address) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    return _bridge.getMACAddress(address->octet);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setHardwareAddress(ether_addr *address)
{
    if (!address) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    return setMacAddress(*address);
}

IO80211Controller *RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getController()
{
    return _controller;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::isChipInterfaceReady()
{
    return !_stopping && _bridge.isAttached();
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::isDebounceOnGoing()
{
    return false;
}

IO80211LinkState RTW88WCLInfraInterface::linkState()
{
    if (!_bridge.isAttached() || !_bridge.connectionReady())
        return kIO80211NetworkLinkDown;
    return kIO80211NetworkLinkUp;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setLinkState(IO80211LinkState state,
                                           unsigned int reason,
                                           bool debounce,
                                           unsigned int duration)
{
    return IO80211InfraInterface::setLinkState(state, reason, debounce, duration);
}

int RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getAssocState()
{
    return linkState() == kIO80211NetworkLinkUp ? 1 : 0;
}

UInt RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getHardwareAssists()
{
    return 0;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::bpfTap(unsigned int direction, unsigned int flags)
{
    (void)direction;
    (void)flags;
    return false;
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::hwConfigNicProxyData(nicproxy_info_s *info)
{
    /* Proxy offload is not implemented; leave the opaque SDK structure alone. */
    (void)info;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getCHANNEL(apple80211_channel_data *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    RTW88BSS bss = {};
    IOReturn status = _bridge.copyCurrentBSS(&bss);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) return status;
    if (bss.channel == 0 || bss.channel > 196) return kIOReturnNotReady;

    bzero(data, sizeof(*data));
    data->version = APPLE80211_VERSION;
    data->channel.version = APPLE80211_VERSION;
    data->channel.channel = bss.channel;
    data->channel.flags = bss.channel <= 14 ? APPLE80211_C_FLAG_2GHZ
                                             : APPLE80211_C_FLAG_5GHZ;
    data->channel.flags |= APPLE80211_C_FLAG_20MHZ;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getRSSI(apple80211_rssi_data *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    int rssi = 0;
    IOReturn status = _bridge.getRSSI(&rssi);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) return status;

    bzero(data, sizeof(*data));
    data->version = APPLE80211_VERSION;
    data->num_radios = 1;
    data->rssi_unit = APPLE80211_UNIT_DBM;
    data->rssi[0] = rssi;
    data->aggregate_rssi = rssi;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::getSUPPORTED_CHANNELS(
    apple80211_sup_channel_data *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;

    RTW88Channel channels[APPLE80211_MAX_CHANNELS] = {};
    uint32_t count = 0;
    IOReturn status = _bridge.copyChannels(channels,
                                           APPLE80211_MAX_CHANNELS, &count);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) return status;
    if (count > APPLE80211_MAX_CHANNELS) return kIOReturnNoResources;

    bzero(data, sizeof(*data));
    data->version = APPLE80211_VERSION;
    data->num_channels = count;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        if (!channels[i].number || channels[i].number > 196)
            return kIOReturnError;
        data->supported_channels[i].version = APPLE80211_VERSION;
        data->supported_channels[i].channel = channels[i].number;
        data->supported_channels[i].flags = channelFlags(channels[i]);
    }
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setCIPHER_KEY(apple80211_key *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;

    /* The Sequoia WCL protocol uses cipher values PMK (6), PMKSA (7) and MSK
     * (9) here. The RTW88 internal WPA2-PSK path consumes its PMK from the
     * association request and performs the four-way handshake itself. The
     * backend has no WCL PMKSA/MSK cache insertion API yet. Do not reinterpret
     * this message as a PTK/GTK key install. */
    return kIOReturnUnsupported;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setCLEAR_PMKSA_CACHE(void *data)
{
    (void)data;
    /* The existing RTW88 backend does not expose a PMKSA-cache removal API. */
    return kIOReturnUnsupported;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_LEAVE_NETWORK(
    apple80211_leave_network *data)
{
    (void)data;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    IOReturn status = _bridge.leaveNetwork();
    if (status == kIOReturnSuccess) {
        /* Invalidate any deferred disconnect callback from the old join. */
        _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
        _connectNotified = false;
        _assocNotified = false;
        setCurrentApAddress(nullptr);
        setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkDown, 0, false, 0);
        RTW88WCLLinkStatusEvent link = {};
        link.up = 0;
        postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageLinkStatus, &link, sizeof(link));
        bzero(_targetBSSID, sizeof(_targetBSSID));
    }
    return status;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_SCAN_ABORT(void *data)
{
    (void)data;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    return _bridge.abortScan();
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_JOIN_ABORT(void *data)
{
    (void)data;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached()) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    IOReturn status = _bridge.abortJoin();
    if (status == kIOReturnSuccess) {
        _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
        _assocNotified = false;
        _connectNotified = false;
        postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageJoinAbortComplete, nullptr, 0);
        setCurrentApAddress(nullptr);
        setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkDown, 0, false, 0);
        bzero(_targetBSSID, sizeof(_targetBSSID));
    }
    return status;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_SCAN_REQ(apple80211ScanRequest *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached() || _stopping) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (_scanActive || _bridge.scanInFlight()) return kIOReturnBusy;

    const uint8_t *raw = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(data);
    const uint32_t channelCount = RTW88WCLRequest::read32(raw + 0x54);
    if (channelCount > 400) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    const size_t requestLength = 0x58 + static_cast<size_t>(channelCount) * 12;
    RTW88WCLRequest::Scan request = {};
    if (!RTW88WCLRequest::decodeScan(data, requestLength, request))
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    /* The backend currently supports active scans only. A FAST request is
     * serviced as a fresh active scan because there is no separate cached-
     * scan transaction; passive/background requests fail closed rather than
     * being silently converted into active probe transmission. */
    if (request.type != APPLE80211_SCAN_TYPE_ACTIVE &&
        request.type != APPLE80211_SCAN_TYPE_FAST)
        return kIOReturnUnsupported;

    uint16_t channels[128] = {};
    uint32_t selected = 0;
    for (unsigned channel = 1; channel <= 196; ++channel) {
        if (!(request.channelBitmap[channel / 8] & (1U << (channel % 8))))
            continue;
        if (selected == sizeof(channels) / sizeof(channels[0]))
            return kIOReturnNoResources;
        channels[selected++] = static_cast<uint16_t>(channel);
    }

    if (request.channelCount != 0 && selected == 0)
        return kIOReturnUnsupported;

    ScanFilter filter = {};
    filter.identifier = request.identifier;
    filter.ssidLength = static_cast<uint8_t>(request.ssidLength);
    memcpy(filter.ssid, request.ssid, sizeof(filter.ssid));
    memcpy(filter.bssid, request.bssid, sizeof(filter.bssid));
    memcpy(filter.channelBitmap, request.channelBitmap,
           sizeof(filter.channelBitmap));
    filter.filterBSSID = !isZeroAddress(filter.bssid, sizeof(filter.bssid));
    filter.filterChannels = request.channelCount != 0;
    _scanFilter = filter;
    _scanActive = true;
    /* The bridge writes this generation before it enters the asynchronous
     * backend, so a fast completion cannot race the interface's filter state. */
    _activeScanGeneration = 0;
    setScanningState(request.identifier, true, nullptr, 0);

    IOReturn status = _bridge.startScan(selected ? channels : nullptr, selected,
                                        &_activeScanGeneration);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        _scanActive = false;
        _activeScanGeneration = 0;
        setScanningState(request.identifier, false, nullptr, 0);
    }
    return status;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_ASSOCIATE(
    apple80211AssocCandidates *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_bridge.isAttached() || _stopping) return kIOReturnNotReady;

    const uint8_t *raw = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(data);
    const uint32_t candidates = RTW88WCLRequest::read32(raw + 0x214);
    if (candidates == 0 || candidates > 10) return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    const size_t requestLength = 0x218 + static_cast<size_t>(candidates) * 18;
    RTW88WCLRequest::Join request = {};
    if (!RTW88WCLRequest::decodeJoin(data, requestLength, request))
        return kIOReturnUnsupported;

    const uint8_t *pmk = request.keyLength == 32 ? request.key : nullptr;
    const uint8_t *rsn = request.rsnLength ? request.rsn : nullptr;
    _assocNotified = false;
    _connectNotified = false;
    memcpy(_targetBSSID, request.bssid, sizeof(_targetBSSID));
    /* The bridge writes this generation before cmdConnect() can schedule
     * authentication or produce an event. Duplicate pending joins reuse the
     * existing generation. */
    _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
    IOReturn status = _bridge.associate(request.ssid,
                                        static_cast<uint8_t>(request.ssidLength),
                                        request.bssid, pmk, rsn,
                                        request.rsnLength, false /* internal RTW88 supplicant */,
                                        &_activeJoinGeneration);
    bzero(request.key, sizeof(request.key));
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
        bzero(_targetBSSID, sizeof(_targetBSSID));
        return status;
    }
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_LINK_UP_DONE(void *data)
{
    (void)data;
    maybePublishConnected();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::setWCL_LINK_STATE_UPDATE(
    apple80211_wcl_update_link_state *data)
{
    if (!data) return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    /* Sequoia 24H32 WCL wire layout: byte 6 indicates an up-state record,
     * byte 8 says the BSSID field is valid, byte 7 indicates link-up, and
     * bytes 12..15 carry the down-state debounce interval. These offsets
     * match the reviewed 24H32 WCL protocol layout; don't cast the opaque
     * private structure to an older apple80211 definition. */
    const uint8_t *bytes = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(data);
    if (bytes[6]) {
        if (bytes[8])
            setCurrentApAddress(reinterpret_cast<ether_addr *>(data));
        if (bytes[7])
            setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkUp, 0, false, 0);
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    }

    const uint32_t debounce = RTW88WCLRequest::read32(bytes + 12);
    setCurrentApAddress(nullptr);
    setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkDown, 0, debounce != 0, debounce);
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::scanResultMatches(const RTW88BSS &bss) const
{
    if (bss.channel == 0 || bss.channel > 196 || bss.ssid_len > 32 ||
        bss.ies_len > sizeof(bss.ies) ||
        !validInformationElements(bss.ies, bss.ies_len)) return false;
    if (_scanFilter.filterBSSID &&
        memcmp(bss.bssid, _scanFilter.bssid, sizeof(_scanFilter.bssid)) != 0)
        return false;
    if (_scanFilter.ssidLength &&
        (bss.ssid_len != _scanFilter.ssidLength ||
         memcmp(bss.ssid, _scanFilter.ssid, _scanFilter.ssidLength) != 0))
        return false;
    if (_scanFilter.filterChannels &&
        !(_scanFilter.channelBitmap[bss.channel / 8] &
          (1U << (bss.channel % 8))))
        return false;
    return true;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLInfraInterface::postWCLMessage(unsigned int message,
                                                 void *payload,
                                                 unsigned long length)
{
    if (!_controller) return kIOReturnNotReady;
    return _controller->postMessage(this, message, payload, length, true);
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::publishScanResults(IOReturn status)
{
    if (!_controller) return;
    if (status == kIOReturnSuccess) {
        for (uint32_t index = 0; index < 128; ++index) {
            RTW88BSS bss = {};
            IOReturn copied = _bridge.copyScanBSS(index, &bss);
            if (copied == kIOReturnNotFound) break;
            if (copied != kIOReturnSuccess) break;
            if (!scanResultMatches(bss)) continue;

            uint8_t message[sizeof(RTW88WCLBeaconMetadata) + sizeof(bss.ies)] = {};
            RTW88WCLBeaconMetadata metadata = {};
            metadata.ieLength = bss.ies_len;
            metadata.channelSpec = static_cast<uint16_t>(bss.channel |
                (bss.channel <= 14 ? 0x1000 : 0xd000));
            memcpy(metadata.ssid, bss.ssid, bss.ssid_len);
            metadata.ssidLength = bss.ssid_len;
            metadata.channel = bss.channel;
            memcpy(metadata.bssid, bss.bssid, sizeof(metadata.bssid));
            metadata.rssi = bss.rssi;
            metadata.interval = bss.beacon_interval;
            metadata.capability = static_cast<uint16_t>(bss.capabilities);
            metadata.flags = kWCLBeaconRSSIValid;
            if (bss.ssid_len) metadata.flags |= kWCLBeaconSSIDPresent;
            memcpy(message, &metadata, sizeof(metadata));
            if (bss.ies_len)
                memcpy(message + sizeof(metadata), bss.ies, bss.ies_len);
            postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageScanResult, message,
                           sizeof(metadata) + bss.ies_len);
        }
    }

    _scanActive = false;
    _activeScanGeneration = 0;
    setScanningState(_scanFilter.identifier, false, nullptr, 0);
    postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageScanDone, &status, sizeof(status));
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::postConnectionComplete(uint16_t status,
                                                     uint16_t reason,
                                                     IOReturn driverStatus)
{
    RTW88WCLConnectionEvent event = {};
    event.status = status;
    event.reason = reason;
    memcpy(event.peers[0].bssid, _targetBSSID, sizeof(_targetBSSID));
    event.peers[0].driverStatus = driverStatus;
    postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageConnectComplete, &event, sizeof(event));
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::maybePublishConnected()
{
    /* setWCL_LINK_UP_DONE may be delivered after a leave/abort request. Never
     * publish an old backend CONNECTED state without a live join transaction. */
    if (!_activeJoinGeneration || _connectNotified || !_bridge.isAttached() ||
        !_bridge.connectionReady())
        return;

    _connectNotified = true;
    _assocNotified = true;
    postConnectionComplete(0, 0, kIOReturnSuccess);
    setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkUp, 0, false, 0);

    RTW88WCLLinkStatusEvent link = {};
    memcpy(link.bssid, _targetBSSID, sizeof(link.bssid));
    link.up = 1;
    postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageLinkStatus, &link, sizeof(link));
}

void RTW88WCLInfraInterface::handleRTW88WCLRadioEvent(
    const RTW88WCLRadioEvent &event)
{
    if (_stopping) return;
    switch (event.event) {
        case kRTW88EventScanDone: {
            if (!_scanActive || event.scanGeneration != _activeScanGeneration)
                return; // stale completion from an aborted/superseded scan
            const IOReturn status = event.value ? kIOReturnAborted
                                                 : kIOReturnSuccess;
            publishScanResults(status);
            break;
        }
        case kRTW88EventConnected: {
            if (!_activeJoinGeneration ||
                event.joinGeneration != _activeJoinGeneration)
                return;
            if (!_assocNotified) {
                RTW88WCLAssociationEvent assoc = {};
                memcpy(assoc.bssid, _targetBSSID, sizeof(assoc.bssid));
                postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageAssocComplete, &assoc, sizeof(assoc));
                _assocNotified = true;
            }
            maybePublishConnected();
            break;
        }
        case kRTW88EventAssocDone: {
            if (!_activeJoinGeneration ||
                event.joinGeneration != _activeJoinGeneration)
                return; // stale association callback from an earlier join
            if (!_assocNotified) {
                RTW88WCLAssociationEvent assoc = {};
                memcpy(assoc.bssid, _targetBSSID, sizeof(assoc.bssid));
                postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageAssocComplete, &assoc, sizeof(assoc));
                _assocNotified = true;
            }
            maybePublishConnected();
            break;
        }
        case kRTW88EventDeauth: {
            if (!_activeJoinGeneration ||
                event.joinGeneration != _activeJoinGeneration)
                return;
            uint32_t reason = event.value;
            postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageDeauth, &reason, sizeof(reason));
            postConnectionComplete(1, static_cast<uint16_t>(reason),
                                   kIOReturnNotAttached);
            _connectNotified = false;
            _assocNotified = false;
            _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
            setCurrentApAddress(nullptr);
            setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkDown, reason, false, 0);
            break;
        }
        case kRTW88EventDisconnected: {
            if (!_activeJoinGeneration ||
                event.joinGeneration != _activeJoinGeneration)
                return;
            if (_connectNotified || _assocNotified)
                postConnectionComplete(1, 0, kIOReturnNotAttached);
            _connectNotified = false;
            _assocNotified = false;
            _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
            setCurrentApAddress(nullptr);
            setLinkState(kIO80211NetworkLinkDown, 0, false, 0);
            RTW88WCLLinkStatusEvent link = {};
            memcpy(link.bssid, _targetBSSID, sizeof(link.bssid));
            link.up = 0;
            postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageLinkStatus, &link, sizeof(link));
            break;
        }
        case kRTW88EventRSSIChanged: {
            if (!_activeJoinGeneration ||
                event.joinGeneration != _activeJoinGeneration)
                return;
            int rssi = 0;
            if (_bridge.getRSSI(&rssi) == kIOReturnSuccess) {
                RTW88WCLLqmUpdate update = {};
                update.rssiValid = 1;
                update.rssi = rssi;
                postWCLMessage(kWCLMessageLqmUpdate, &update, sizeof(update));
            }
            break;
        }
    }
}

#undef super

    IOReturn attachRadio(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio);
    void detachRadio();
    RTW88WCLRadioBridge *radioBridge() { return &_bridge; }

private:
    struct ScanFilter {
        uint32_t identifier;
        uint8_t ssid[32];
        uint8_t ssidLength;

    void clearRegistrationInfo();

                                IOReturn driverStatus);
    void maybePublishConnected();
    IOReturn postWCLMessage(unsigned int message, void *payload,
                            unsigned long length);

    RTW88WCLController *_controller = nullptr; // provider-owned
    RTW88WCLRadioBridge _bridge;
    RTW88WCLWorkQueue *_workQueue = nullptr;   // interface-owned
    ScanFilter _scanFilter = {};
    uint8_t _targetBSSID[6] = {0};
    bool _scanActive = false;
    uint32_t _activeScanGeneration = 0;
    uint32_t _activeJoinGeneration = 0;
    bool _assocNotified = false;
    bool _connectNotified = false;
    bool _stopping = false;

    /* Opaque 24H32 payload storage must outlive any future registration. The
     * builder is staged but not called from start() until queue/pool ownership
     * and registration rollback have been verified.
     */
    RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32::Storage _registrationInfo = {};
    bool _registrationInfoPrepared = false;
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Fixed-capacity lock-free MPMC ring for owned pointers (mbufs or Skywalk packets).
 *
 * The ring does not allocate after reset(). It is safe for concurrent producers
 * and one or more consumers. reset() is lifecycle-only and must run after every
 * producer and consumer has been quiesced. A successful push transfers pointer
 * ownership to the ring; a failed push leaves ownership with the caller.
 */
#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

// The bounded sequence algorithm uses a power-of-two capacity for masking.
template <typename T, uint32_t Capacity>
class RTW88WCLMbufRing final {
    static_assert(Capacity >= 2, "RX ring capacity must be at least two");
    static_assert((Capacity & (Capacity - 1)) == 0,
                  "RX ring capacity must be a power of two");

    struct Cell {
        volatile size_t sequence;
        T value;
    };

public:
    void reset()
    {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
            _cells[i].value = T{};
            __atomic_store_n(&_cells[i].sequence, i, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        }
        __atomic_store_n(&_enqueuePosition, 0, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        __atomic_store_n(&_dequeuePosition, 0, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    }

    bool push(const T &value)
    {
        if (!value)
            return false;

        size_t position = __atomic_load_n(&_enqueuePosition, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        Cell *cell = nullptr;
        for (;;) {
            cell = &_cells[position & (Capacity - 1)];
            const size_t sequence = __atomic_load_n(&cell->sequence,
                                                    __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
            const intptr_t difference = static_cast<intptr_t>(sequence) -
                                       static_cast<intptr_t>(position);
            if (difference == 0) {
                size_t expected = position;
                if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_enqueuePosition, &expected,
                                                position + 1, false,
                                                __ATOMIC_RELAXED,
                                                __ATOMIC_RELAXED))
                    break;
                position = expected;
            } else if (difference < 0) {
                return false; // bounded queue full; caller keeps ownership
            } else {
                position = __atomic_load_n(&_enqueuePosition,
                                           __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
            }
        }

        cell->value = value;
        __atomic_store_n(&cell->sequence, position + 1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        return true;
    }

    bool pop(T *out)
    {
        if (!out)
            return false;

        size_t position = __atomic_load_n(&_dequeuePosition, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        Cell *cell = nullptr;
        for (;;) {
            cell = &_cells[position & (Capacity - 1)];
            const size_t sequence = __atomic_load_n(&cell->sequence,
                                                    __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
            const intptr_t difference = static_cast<intptr_t>(sequence) -
                                       static_cast<intptr_t>(position + 1);
            if (difference == 0) {
                size_t expected = position;
                if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_dequeuePosition, &expected,
                                                position + 1, false,
                                                __ATOMIC_RELAXED,
                                                __ATOMIC_RELAXED))
                    break;
                position = expected;
            } else if (difference < 0) {
                return false; // empty or a producer has not published yet
            } else {
                position = __atomic_load_n(&_dequeuePosition,
                                           __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
            }
        }

        *out = cell->value;
        cell->value = T{};
        __atomic_store_n(&cell->sequence, position + Capacity,
                         __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        return true;
    }

    bool empty() const
    {
        const size_t position = __atomic_load_n(&_dequeuePosition,
                                                __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        const Cell *cell = &_cells[position & (Capacity - 1)];
        const size_t sequence = __atomic_load_n(&cell->sequence,
                                                __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
        const intptr_t difference = static_cast<intptr_t>(sequence) -
                                   static_cast<intptr_t>(position + 1);
        return difference < 0;
    }

    uint32_t approximateSize() const
    {
        const size_t enqueued = __atomic_load_n(&_enqueuePosition,
                                               __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
        const size_t dequeued = __atomic_load_n(&_dequeuePosition,
                                                __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
        const size_t count = enqueued >= dequeued ? enqueued - dequeued : 0;
        return count > Capacity ? Capacity : static_cast<uint32_t>(count);
    }

    static constexpr uint32_t capacity() { return Capacity; }

private:
    alignas(64) volatile size_t _enqueuePosition = 0;
    alignas(64) volatile size_t _dequeuePosition = 0;
    alignas(64) Cell _cells[Capacity] = {};
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * ABI-neutral PCI/MMIO/DMA owner for the native Sequoia WCL frontend.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLPCIBackend.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.h>
#include <kern/thread.h>
#include <string.h>

extern "C" {
#include "../../compat/rtw88_compat.h"
void rtw88_trigger_interrupt(void);
boolean_t preemption_enabled(void);
}

#define super OSObject
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLPCIBackend, OSObject)

bool RTW88WCLPCIBackend::init()
{
    if (!super::init())
        return false;
    _pciDev = nullptr;
    _mmioMap = nullptr;
    _mmioBase = nullptr;
    _interruptSource = nullptr;
    _workLoop = nullptr;
    _compatPciDev = nullptr;
    _client = nullptr;
    _dmaLock = nullptr;
    _pendingFreeLock = nullptr;
    _dmaList = nullptr;
    _dmaPendingFree = nullptr;
    _compatLeaseHeld = false;
    _compatInitialized = false;
    _opened = false;
    _radioRunning = false;
    return true;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLPCIBackend::open(IOPCIDevice *provider,
                                  IOWorkLoop *workLoop,
                                  RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient *client)
{
    if (!provider || !workLoop)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (_opened || _pciDev || _compatLeaseHeld)
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    _pciDev = provider;
    _pciDev->retain();
    _workLoop = workLoop;
    _workLoop->retain();
    _client = client;

    _dmaLock = IOSimpleLockAlloc();
    _pendingFreeLock = IOSimpleLockAlloc();
    if (!_dmaLock || !_pendingFreeLock) {
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnNoMemory;
    }

    _pciDev->setMemoryEnable(true);
    _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(true);
    const UInt16 enabledCommand = _pciDev->configRead16(kIOPCIConfigCommand);
    if ((enabledCommand & 0x0006U) != 0x0006U) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI memory/bus-master enable failed (command=0x%04x)\n",
              enabledCommand);
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnNotReady;
    }

    /* rtw88's PCI backend maps BAR2 (the Linux driver uses bar_id=2). */
    _mmioMap = _pciDev->mapDeviceMemoryWithRegister(kIOPCIConfigBaseAddress2);
    if (!_mmioMap || !_mmioMap->getVirtualAddress() || !_mmioMap->getLength()) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: unable to map a usable BAR2\n");
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnNoResources;
    }
    _mmioBase = reinterpret_cast<volatile void *>(_mmioMap->getVirtualAddress());

    _compatPciDev = static_cast<struct pci_dev *>(IOMallocZero(sizeof(*_compatPciDev)));
    if (!_compatPciDev) {
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnNoMemory;
    }
    _compatPciDev->vendor = _pciDev->configRead16(0x00);
    _compatPciDev->device = _pciDev->configRead16(0x02);
    _compatPciDev->kext_dev = this;
    _compatPciDev->resource[2] = reinterpret_cast<resource_size_t>(_mmioBase);
    _compatPciDev->resource_len[2] = static_cast<resource_size_t>(_mmioMap->getLength());

    /* The compat layer has process-global callbacks, so acquire this lease
     * before publishing the function tables or starting its workqueues. */
    RTW88HwOps *expectedOwner = nullptr;
    if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&g_pci_dev_instance, &expectedOwner,
                                     static_cast<RTW88HwOps *>(this), false,
                                     __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: RTW88 compat hardware lease is already owned\n");
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnBusy;
    }
    _compatLeaseHeld = true;
    rtw88_pci_io_ops = &_pci_io_ops;
    rtw88_dma_ops = &_dma_ops;

    const int compatStatus = rtw88_compat_init();
    if (compatStatus != 0) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: compat runtime initialization failed (%d)\n",
              compatStatus);
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnNoResources;
    }
    _compatInitialized = true;

    _interruptSource = IOInterruptEventSource::interruptEventSource(
        this,
        OSMemberFunctionCast(IOInterruptEventSource::Action, this,
                             &RTW88WCLPCIBackend::handleInterrupt),
        _pciDev, 0);
    if (!_interruptSource ||
        _workLoop->addEventSource(_interruptSource) != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: unable to register PCI interrupt event source\n");
        closeResources();
        return kIOReturnNoResources;
    }
    _interruptSource->disable();

    _opened = true;
    IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI backend acquired %04x:%04x, BAR2=%p length=0x%llx\n",
          _compatPciDev->vendor, _compatPciDev->device, (void *)_mmioBase,
          static_cast<unsigned long long>(_mmioMap->getLength()));
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLPCIBackend::setRadioRunning(bool running)
{
    if (!_opened || !_pciDev || !_interruptSource)
        return kIOReturnNotReady;
    if (_radioRunning == running)
        return kIOReturnSuccess;

    if (running) {
        _pciDev->setMemoryEnable(true);
        _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(true);
        const UInt16 command = _pciDev->configRead16(kIOPCIConfigCommand);
        if ((command & 0x0006U) != 0x0006U) {
            _interruptSource->disable();
            _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(false);
            const UInt16 rollbackCommand = _pciDev->configRead16(kIOPCIConfigCommand);
            if (rollbackCommand & 0x0004U) {
                /* Bus mastering may still be active. Keep the backend marked
                 * running so close/free cannot reclaim DMA mappings. */
                _radioRunning = true;
                IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: partial PCI enable rollback failed (command=0x%04x); DMA resources quarantined\n",
                      rollbackCommand);
            } else {
                _radioRunning = false;
                _pciDev->setMemoryEnable(false);
            }
            return kIOReturnNotReady;
        }
        _radioRunning = true;
        _interruptSource->enable();
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    }

    /* Caller contract: RTW88IEEE80211::stop() must already have synchronized
     * its interrupt/NAPI workers and stopped device DMA before this call. */
    _interruptSource->disable();
    _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(false);
    const UInt16 command = _pciDev->configRead16(kIOPCIConfigCommand);
    if (command & 0x0004U) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI bus-master fence did not stick (command=0x%04x)\n",
              command);
        return kIOReturnNotReady;
    }
    _radioRunning = false;
    if (preemption_enabled())
        drainPendingFree();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLPCIBackend::close()
{
    if (_radioRunning)
        return kIOReturnBusy;
    closeResources();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::closeResources()
{
    if (_interruptSource) {
        _interruptSource->disable();
        if (_workLoop)
            _workLoop->removeEventSource(_interruptSource);
        _interruptSource->release();
        _interruptSource = nullptr;
    }

    /* Stop all compat work before reclaiming DMA buffers and releasing the
     * callbacks they may invoke. The RTW88 core must already be stopped. */
    if (_compatInitialized) {
        rtw88_compat_exit();
        _compatInitialized = false;
    }

    if (preemption_enabled())
        drainPendingFree();
    releaseDMAEntries();

    clearCompatLease();

    if (_pciDev) {
        _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(false);
        _pciDev->setMemoryEnable(false);
    }
    if (_compatPciDev) {
        IOFree(_compatPciDev, sizeof(*_compatPciDev));
        _compatPciDev = nullptr;
    }
    if (_mmioMap) {
        _mmioMap->release();
        _mmioMap = nullptr;
    }
    _mmioBase = nullptr;

    if (_workLoop) {
        _workLoop->release();
        _workLoop = nullptr;
    }
    if (_pciDev) {
        _pciDev->release();
        _pciDev = nullptr;
    }
    if (_pendingFreeLock) {
        IOSimpleLockFree(_pendingFreeLock);
        _pendingFreeLock = nullptr;
    }
    if (_dmaLock) {
        IOSimpleLockFree(_dmaLock);
        _dmaLock = nullptr;
    }

    _client = nullptr;
    _radioRunning = false;
    _opened = false;
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::clearCompatLease()
{
    if (!_compatLeaseHeld)
        return;
    RTW88HwOps *expectedOwner = static_cast<RTW88HwOps *>(this);
    if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&g_pci_dev_instance, &expectedOwner,
                                    static_cast<RTW88HwOps *>(nullptr), false,
                                    __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) {
        rtw88_pci_io_ops = nullptr;
        rtw88_dma_ops = nullptr;
    }
    _compatLeaseHeld = false;
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::free()
{
    if (_pciDev) {
        if (_interruptSource)
            _interruptSource->disable();
        _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(false);
        const UInt16 command = _pciDev->configRead16(kIOPCIConfigCommand);
        if (command & 0x0004U) {
            IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: PCI bus-master bit remains set (command=0x%04x); refusing to free DMA resources; backend quarantined\n",
                  command);
            /* Keep this object, DMA descriptors, MMIO map, locks, and PCI
             * reference alive until hardware bus mastering is demonstrably off. */
            return;
        }
    }
    _radioRunning = false;
    closeResources();
    super::free();
}

uint64_t RTW88WCLPCIBackend::mmioLength() const
{
    return _mmioMap ? static_cast<uint64_t>(_mmioMap->getLength()) : 0;
}

UInt8 RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciReadByte(int offset)
{
    return _pciDev ? _pciDev->configRead8(static_cast<UInt8>(offset)) : 0xff;
}

UInt16 RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciReadWord(int offset)
{
    return _pciDev ? _pciDev->configRead16(static_cast<UInt8>(offset)) : 0xffff;
}

UInt32 RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciReadDword(int offset)
{
    return _pciDev ? _pciDev->configRead32(static_cast<UInt8>(offset)) : 0xffffffffU;
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciWriteByte(int offset, UInt8 value)
{
    if (_pciDev) _pciDev->configWrite8(static_cast<UInt8>(offset), value);
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciWriteWord(int offset, UInt16 value)
{
    if (_pciDev) _pciDev->configWrite16(static_cast<UInt8>(offset), value);
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciWriteDword(int offset, UInt32 value)
{
    if (_pciDev) _pciDev->configWrite32(static_cast<UInt8>(offset), value);
}

int RTW88WCLPCIBackend::pciFindCapability(int capability)
{
    if (!_pciDev || capability < 0 || capability > 0xff)
        return 0;
    UInt8 offset = 0;
    return _pciDev->findPCICapability(static_cast<UInt8>(capability), &offset)
               ? static_cast<int>(offset) : 0;
}

void *RTW88WCLPCIBackend::allocCoherent(size_t size,
                                         IOPhysicalAddress *physical)
{
    if (physical) *physical = 0;
    if (!size || !_compatLeaseHeld || !_dmaLock || !_pendingFreeLock)
        return nullptr;
    if (preemption_enabled())
        drainPendingFree();

    IOBufferMemoryDescriptor *descriptor =
        IOBufferMemoryDescriptor::inTaskWithPhysicalMask(
            kernel_task,
            kIOMemoryPhysicallyContiguous | kIODirectionInOut |
                kIOMemoryKernelUserShared,
            size, 0x00000000FFFFFFF0ULL);
    if (!descriptor)
        return nullptr;
    if (descriptor->prepare() != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        descriptor->release();
        return nullptr;
    }

    const IOPhysicalAddress address = descriptor->getPhysicalAddress();
    void *virtualAddress = descriptor->getBytesNoCopy();
    if (!virtualAddress || !address) {
        descriptor->complete();
        descriptor->release();
        return nullptr;
    }
    bzero(virtualAddress, size);

    RTW88DMAEntry *entry = static_cast<RTW88DMAEntry *>(IOMallocZero(sizeof(*entry)));
    if (!entry) {
        descriptor->complete();
        descriptor->release();
        return nullptr;
    }
    entry->desc = descriptor;
    entry->virt = virtualAddress;
    entry->phys = address;
    entry->size = size;

    IOSimpleLockLock(_dmaLock);
    entry->next = _dmaList;
    _dmaList = entry;
    IOSimpleLockUnlock(_dmaLock);
    if (physical) *physical = address;
    return virtualAddress;
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::freeCoherent(size_t size, void *virtualAddress,
                                      IOPhysicalAddress physical)
{
    (void)size;
    (void)physical;
    if (!_dmaLock || !_pendingFreeLock || !virtualAddress)
        return;

    IOSimpleLockLock(_dmaLock);
    RTW88DMAEntry **previous = &_dmaList;
    RTW88DMAEntry *entry = _dmaList;
    while (entry && entry->virt != virtualAddress) {
        previous = &entry->next;
        entry = entry->next;
    }
    if (entry)
        *previous = entry->next;
    IOSimpleLockUnlock(_dmaLock);

    if (!entry) {
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: freeCoherent: unknown virtual address %p\n",
              virtualAddress);
        return;
    }
    if (preemption_enabled()) {
        freeDMAList(entry);
    } else {
        IOSimpleLockLock(_pendingFreeLock);
        entry->next = _dmaPendingFree;
        _dmaPendingFree = entry;
        IOSimpleLockUnlock(_pendingFreeLock);
    }
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::freeCoherentByPhys(IOPhysicalAddress physical)
{
    if (!_dmaLock || !_pendingFreeLock || !physical)
        return;

    IOSimpleLockLock(_dmaLock);
    RTW88DMAEntry **previous = &_dmaList;
    RTW88DMAEntry *entry = _dmaList;
    while (entry && entry->phys != physical) {
        previous = &entry->next;
        entry = entry->next;
    }
    if (entry)
        *previous = entry->next;
    IOSimpleLockUnlock(_dmaLock);

    if (!entry)
        return;
    if (preemption_enabled()) {
        freeDMAList(entry);
    } else {
        IOSimpleLockLock(_pendingFreeLock);
        entry->next = _dmaPendingFree;
        _dmaPendingFree = entry;
        IOSimpleLockUnlock(_pendingFreeLock);
    }
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::setBounceOrigVA(IOPhysicalAddress physical,
                                         void *originalAddress)
{
    if (!_dmaLock || !physical)
        return;
    IOSimpleLockLock(_dmaLock);
    for (RTW88DMAEntry *entry = _dmaList; entry; entry = entry->next) {
        if (entry->phys == physical) {
            entry->orig_va = originalAddress;
            break;
        }
    }
    IOSimpleLockUnlock(_dmaLock);
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::syncBounceForCpu(IOPhysicalAddress dmaAddress,
                                          size_t size)
{
    if (!_dmaLock || !dmaAddress || !size)
        return;
    /* Keep the entry pinned by the list lock for the bounded copy; this avoids
     * racing a concurrent unmap/free against the original buffer pointer. */
    IOSimpleLockLock(_dmaLock);
    for (RTW88DMAEntry *entry = _dmaList; entry; entry = entry->next) {
        if (entry->phys == dmaAddress && entry->orig_va && entry->virt) {
            const size_t copyLength = size < entry->size ? size : entry->size;
            memcpy(entry->orig_va, entry->virt, copyLength);
            break;
        }
    }
    IOSimpleLockUnlock(_dmaLock);
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::resumeTxIfStalled()
{
    if (_client)
        _client->rtw88WCLResumeTransmit();
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::handleInterrupt(IOInterruptEventSource *source,
                                          int count)
{
    (void)source;
    (void)count;
    if (!_radioRunning || !g_pci_dev_instance ||
        __atomic_load_n(&g_pci_dev_instance, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) !=
            static_cast<RTW88HwOps *>(this))
        return;
    if (preemption_enabled())
        drainPendingFree();
    rtw88_trigger_interrupt();
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::drainPendingFree()
{
    if (!_pendingFreeLock)
        return;
    IOSimpleLockLock(_pendingFreeLock);
    RTW88DMAEntry *list = _dmaPendingFree;
    _dmaPendingFree = nullptr;
    IOSimpleLockUnlock(_pendingFreeLock);
    freeDMAList(list);
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::releaseDMAEntries()
{
    if (_dmaLock) {
        IOSimpleLockLock(_dmaLock);
        RTW88DMAEntry *list = _dmaList;
        _dmaList = nullptr;
        IOSimpleLockUnlock(_dmaLock);
        freeDMAList(list);
    }
    if (_pendingFreeLock) {
        IOSimpleLockLock(_pendingFreeLock);
        RTW88DMAEntry *pending = _dmaPendingFree;
        _dmaPendingFree = nullptr;
        IOSimpleLockUnlock(_pendingFreeLock);
        freeDMAList(pending);
    }
}

void RTW88WCLPCIBackend::freeDMAList(RTW88DMAEntry *list)
{
    while (list) {
        RTW88DMAEntry *next = list->next;
        if (list->desc) {
            list->desc->complete();
            list->desc->release();
        }
        IOFree(list, sizeof(*list));
        list = next;
    }
}

#undef super

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * ABI-neutral PCI/MMIO/DMA owner for the native Sequoia WCL frontend.
 *
 * This class intentionally knows nothing about IO80211Controller or any
 * IO80211Family ABI. It owns the IOKit PCI resources consumed by the common
 * RTW88 core and exposes them through RTW88HwOps. The native controller owns
 * this object and must stop RTW88IEEE80211 before setRadioRunning(false) and
 * close().
 */
#pragma once

#include "../RTW88IEEE80211.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOBufferMemoryDescriptor.h>
#include <IOKit/IOInterruptEventSource.h>
#include <IOKit/IOWorkLoop.h>
#include <IOKit/IOMemoryDescriptor.h>
#include <IOKit/IOLocks.h>
#include <IOKit/pci/IOPCIDevice.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSObject.h>

class RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient {
public:
    virtual void rtw88WCLResumeTransmit() = 0;
    virtual ~RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient() {}
};

class RTW88WCLPCIBackend final : public OSObject, public RTW88HwOps {
    OSDeclareDefaultStructors(RTW88WCLPCIBackend)

public:
    bool init() override;
    void free() override;

    /* Acquires one process-wide RTW88 compat-hardware lease. This is
     * deliberately exclusive: the legacy controller and a native WCL
     * controller must never drive the same global rtw88 compat callbacks. */
    IOReturn open(IOPCIDevice *provider, IOWorkLoop *workLoop,
                  RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient *client = nullptr);

    /* The caller must first stop RTW88IEEE80211 so it can no longer issue
     * MMIO/DMA operations. close() refuses to tear down a running radio. */
    IOReturn close();
    IOReturn setRadioRunning(bool running);

    bool isOpen() const { return _opened; }
    bool isRadioRunning() const { return _radioRunning; }
    IOPCIDevice *pciDevice() const { return _pciDev; } // borrowed
    struct pci_dev *compatPCIDevice() const { return _compatPciDev; } // borrowed
    uint64_t mmioLength() const;

    UInt8 pciReadByte(int offset) override;
    UInt16 pciReadWord(int offset) override;
    UInt32 pciReadDword(int offset) override;
    void pciWriteByte(int offset, UInt8 value) override;
    void pciWriteWord(int offset, UInt16 value) override;
    void pciWriteDword(int offset, UInt32 value) override;
    int pciFindCapability(int capability) override;
    volatile void *mmioBase() const override { return _mmioBase; }
    void *allocCoherent(size_t size, IOPhysicalAddress *physical) override;
    void freeCoherent(size_t size, void *virtualAddress,
                      IOPhysicalAddress physical) override;
    void freeCoherentByPhys(IOPhysicalAddress physical) override;
    void setBounceOrigVA(IOPhysicalAddress physical, void *originalAddress) override;
    void syncBounceForCpu(IOPhysicalAddress dmaAddress, size_t size) override;
    void resumeTxIfStalled() override;

private:
    static void freeDMAList(RTW88DMAEntry *list);
    void drainPendingFree();
    void releaseDMAEntries();
    void handleInterrupt(IOInterruptEventSource *source, int count);
    void clearCompatLease();
    void closeResources();

    IOPCIDevice *_pciDev = nullptr;                // retained
    IOMemoryMap *_mmioMap = nullptr;               // retained
    volatile void *_mmioBase = nullptr;
    IOInterruptEventSource *_interruptSource = nullptr;
    IOWorkLoop *_workLoop = nullptr;               // retained
    struct pci_dev *_compatPciDev = nullptr;
    RTW88WCLPCIBackendClient *_client = nullptr;   // non-owning

    IOSimpleLock *_dmaLock = nullptr;
    IOSimpleLock *_pendingFreeLock = nullptr;
    RTW88DMAEntry *_dmaList = nullptr;
    RTW88DMAEntry *_dmaPendingFree = nullptr;

    bool _compatLeaseHeld = false;
    bool _compatInitialized = false;
    bool _opened = false;
    bool _radioRunning = false;
};


=== FILE driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLPacketSpanValidation.hpp SHA256=06c63ea6dbad3f33a99477c4b4ede09f7c6520fad7437e398b77e75562f06347 BYTES=2176 ===

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Overflow-safe bounds checks shared by native Skywalk TX and RX adapters.
 * This file intentionally has no IOKit dependency so the arithmetic can be
 * exhaustively tested on the host before it is used with private ABI objects.
 */
#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>

struct RTW88WCLPacketSpan {
    uint64_t segmentLength;
    uint64_t bufferOffset;
    uint32_t bufferCapacity;
    uint32_t dataOffset;
    uint32_t dataLength;
};

static inline bool RTW88WCLRangeFits(uint64_t extent,
                                     uint64_t offset,
                                     uint64_t length)
{
    return offset <= extent && length <= (extent - offset);
}

static inline bool RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid(const RTW88WCLPacketSpan &span)
{
    if (span.bufferCapacity == 0)
        return false;
    if (!RTW88WCLRangeFits(span.segmentLength, span.bufferOffset,
                           span.bufferCapacity))
        return false;
    if (!RTW88WCLRangeFits(span.bufferCapacity, span.dataOffset,
                           span.dataLength))
        return false;
    return true;
}

static inline bool RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(uint32_t length,
                                                       uint32_t bufferCapacity)
{
    /* Match RTW88IEEE80211::txDataFrame(): Ethernet header through 2048 bytes. */
    return length >= 14 && length <= 2048 && length <= bufferCapacity;
}

static inline bool RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(
    uint64_t segmentBase, const RTW88WCLPacketSpan &span,
    uintptr_t *address)
{
    if (!address || segmentBase == 0 ||
        !RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid(span))
        return false;

    if (span.bufferOffset > UINT64_MAX - span.dataOffset)
        return false;
    const uint64_t relative = span.bufferOffset + span.dataOffset;
    if (segmentBase > UINT64_MAX - relative)
        return false;

    const uint64_t candidate = segmentBase + relative;
    if (candidate > static_cast<uint64_t>(UINTPTR_MAX) ||
        span.dataLength > static_cast<uint64_t>(UINTPTR_MAX) - candidate)
        return false;

    *address = static_cast<uintptr_t>(candidate);
    return true;
}


=== FILE driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLPacketView.hpp SHA256=e8e205f65c1a764ca65f9f8cd25e1306ad780b7400f7672d4bc9b344998acbad BYTES=2843 ===
SKYWALK_INTEGRATION_LINES: 2,6,7,8,10,12,18,19,20,21,29,34,38,42,59,62,79,80,81,82,84,86

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Checked view over a single-buffer IOSkywalk network packet.
 */
#pragma once

#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkNetworkPacket.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkPacketBuffer.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkMemorySegment.h>

#include "RTW88WCLPacketSpanValidation.hpp"

struct RTW88WCLPacketView {
    const uint8_t *bytes;
    uint32_t length;
    uint32_t capacity;
};

static inline bool RTW88WCLResolveSingleBufferPacket(
    IOSkywalkPacket *packet,
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *expectedPool,
    RTW88WCLPacketView *view)
{
    if (!packet || !expectedPool || !view)
        return false;
    view->bytes = nullptr;
    view->length = 0;
    view->capacity = 0;

    if (packet->getPacketType() != kIOSkywalkPacketTypeNetwork ||
        packet->getPacketBufferPool() != expectedPool ||
        packet->getPacketBufferCount() != 1)
        return false;

    IOSkywalkPacketBuffer *buffers[1] = { nullptr };
    if (packet->getPacketBuffers(buffers, 1) != 1 || !buffers[0])
        return false;

    IOSkywalkPacketBuffer *buffer = buffers[0];
    if (buffer->getPacketBufferPool() != expectedPool)
        return false;

    IOSkywalkMemorySegment *segment = buffer->getMemorySegment();
    if (!segment)
        return false;
    IOMemoryDescriptor *memory = segment->getMemoryDescriptor();
    if (!memory)
        return false;

    const uint64_t segmentLength = memory->getLength();
    const uint64_t segmentBase = segment->getVirtualAddress();
    const uint64_t bufferOffset = buffer->getMemorySegmentOffset();
    const uint32_t capacity = buffer->getBufferSize();
    const uint32_t dataOffset = buffer->getDataOffset();
    const uint32_t dataLength = buffer->getDataLength();
    if (packet->getDataOffset() != dataOffset ||
        packet->getDataLength() != dataLength)
        return false;

    const RTW88WCLPacketSpan span = {
        segmentLength, bufferOffset, capacity, dataOffset, dataLength
    };
    if (!RTW88WCLPacketSpanIsValid(span) || segmentBase == 0)
        return false;

    const uint64_t relativeOffset = bufferOffset + dataOffset;
    if (segmentBase > UINT64_MAX - relativeOffset)
        return false;
    const uint64_t address = segmentBase + relativeOffset;
    if (address > (uint64_t)UINTPTR_MAX)
        return false;

    view->bytes = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(
        static_cast<uintptr_t>(address));
    view->length = dataLength;
    view->capacity = capacity - dataOffset;
    return true;
}

static inline bool RTW88WCLResolveEthernetTxPacket(
    IOSkywalkPacket *packet,
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *expectedPool,
    RTW88WCLPacketView *view)
{
    if (!RTW88WCLResolveSingleBufferPacket(packet, expectedPool, view))
        return false;
    return RTW88WCLIsValidEthernetFrameLength(view->length, view->capacity);
}

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Opaque IOSkywalk RegistrationInfo storage and the verified Apple 24H32
 * post-initializer writes. Field semantics intentionally remain unnamed.
 */
#pragma once

#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>

namespace RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32 {

constexpr size_t kSize = 0x130;
constexpr uint64_t kVersionAndSize = 0x0000013000000001ULL;

/* Offsets/values observed in the 24H32 createSkywalkInterface producer.
 * These are deliberately described as opaque slots, not guessed fields.
 */
constexpr size_t kOpaqueSlot0C = 0x0c;
constexpr size_t kOpaqueSlot14 = 0x14;
constexpr size_t kOpaqueSlot24 = 0x24;
constexpr size_t kOpaqueSlot40 = 0x40;
constexpr size_t kHardwareAddress = 0x108;

constexpr uint32_t kOpaqueValue0C = 3;
constexpr uint32_t kOpaqueValue14 = 2;
constexpr uint64_t kOpaqueValue24 = 0x0000008000000080ULL;
constexpr uint32_t kOpaqueValue40 = 0;

struct alignas(8) Storage {
    uint8_t bytes[kSize];
};
static_assert(sizeof(Storage) == kSize, "24H32 RegistrationInfo storage size");
static_assert(alignof(Storage) >= 8, "24H32 RegistrationInfo alignment");
static_assert(kOpaqueSlot0C + sizeof(uint32_t) <= kSize,
              "24H32 opaque slot 0x0c is in bounds");
static_assert(kOpaqueSlot14 + sizeof(uint32_t) <= kSize,
              "24H32 opaque slot 0x14 is in bounds");
static_assert(kOpaqueSlot24 + sizeof(uint64_t) <= kSize,
              "24H32 opaque slot 0x24 is in bounds");
static_assert(kOpaqueSlot40 + sizeof(uint32_t) <= kSize,
              "24H32 opaque slot 0x40 is in bounds");
static_assert(kHardwareAddress + 6 <= kSize,
              "24H32 hardware address is in bounds");

inline void clear(Storage &storage)
{
    memset(storage.bytes, 0, sizeof(storage.bytes));
}

inline void storeU32(Storage &storage, size_t offset, uint32_t value)
{
    memcpy(storage.bytes + offset, &value, sizeof(value));
}

inline void storeU64(Storage &storage, size_t offset, uint64_t value)
{
    memcpy(storage.bytes + offset, &value, sizeof(value));
}

inline uint64_t loadU64(const Storage &storage, size_t offset)
{
    uint64_t value = 0;
    memcpy(&value, storage.bytes + offset, sizeof(value));
    return value;
}

inline uint32_t loadU32(const Storage &storage, size_t offset)
{
    uint32_t value = 0;
    memcpy(&value, storage.bytes + offset, sizeof(value));
    return value;
}

/* Apply only the fields written by Apple's 24H32 createSkywalkInterface
 * caller after initRegistrationInfo(). The initializer itself remains a
 * separate kernel call because it populates other values from virtual hooks.
 */
inline bool applyAppleProducerWrites(Storage &storage,
                                     const uint8_t hardwareAddress[6])
{
    if (!hardwareAddress)
        return false;

    storeU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot14, kOpaqueValue14);
    storeU64(storage, kOpaqueSlot24, kOpaqueValue24);
    storeU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot0C, kOpaqueValue0C);
    storeU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot40, kOpaqueValue40);
    memcpy(storage.bytes + kHardwareAddress, hardwareAddress, 6);
    return true;
}

/* Called only after the kernel initRegistrationInfo() method has returned
 * true and applyAppleProducerWrites() has run.
 */
inline bool matchesApple24H32Template(const Storage &storage,
                                      const uint8_t hardwareAddress[6])
{
    if (!hardwareAddress)
        return false;
    if (loadU64(storage, 0) != kVersionAndSize)
        return false;
    if (loadU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot0C) != kOpaqueValue0C)
        return false;
    if (loadU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot14) != kOpaqueValue14)
        return false;
    if (loadU64(storage, kOpaqueSlot24) != kOpaqueValue24)
        return false;
    if (loadU32(storage, kOpaqueSlot40) != kOpaqueValue40)
        return false;
    return memcmp(storage.bytes + kHardwareAddress, hardwareAddress, 6) == 0;
}

} // namespace RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32


=== FILE driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLRequestDecoder.hpp SHA256=6f473988ab5c918ebc40ec3b731bf49e01cd6408e65894200031792a14f0c9b2 BYTES=5110 ===

SAFETY_FENCES_LINES: 33,39,98,106,115,116,133
----- COMPLETE SOURCE -----
/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Bounds-checked decoder for the Sequoia 24H32 WCL scan/join request records.
 * The input format is little-endian and is intentionally independent of IOKit.
 */
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <string.h>

namespace RTW88WCLRequest {
static inline uint16_t read16(const uint8_t *p) {
    return static_cast<uint16_t>(p[0]) |
           static_cast<uint16_t>(static_cast<uint16_t>(p[1]) << 8);
}
static inline uint32_t read32(const uint8_t *p) {
    return static_cast<uint32_t>(p[0]) |
           (static_cast<uint32_t>(p[1]) << 8) |
           (static_cast<uint32_t>(p[2]) << 16) |
           (static_cast<uint32_t>(p[3]) << 24);
}

struct Scan {
    uint32_t identifier;
    uint32_t type;
    uint8_t ssid[32];
    uint32_t ssidLength;
    uint8_t bssid[6];
    uint32_t channelCount;
    uint8_t channelBitmap[32];
};

inline bool decodeScan(const void *buffer, size_t length, Scan &out) {
    if (!buffer || length < 0x58) return false;
    const uint8_t *p = static_cast<const uint8_t *>(buffer);
    const uint32_t count = read32(p + 0x54);
    const uint32_t ssidLength = read32(p + 0x1c);
    const uint32_t type = read32(p + 0x40);
    if (count > 400 || ssidLength > 32 || type < 1 || type > 4 ||
        length < 0x58 + static_cast<size_t>(count) * 12) return false;

    Scan candidate = {};
    candidate.identifier = read32(p);
    candidate.type = type;
    candidate.ssidLength = ssidLength;
    candidate.channelCount = count;
    memcpy(candidate.ssid, p + 0x20, ssidLength);
    memcpy(candidate.bssid, p + 0x14, sizeof(candidate.bssid));
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        const uint8_t *entry = p + 0x58 + static_cast<size_t>(i) * 12;
        const uint32_t channel = read32(entry + 4);
        const uint32_t flags = read32(entry + 8);
        if (channel == 0 || channel > 196 || (flags & 0x2000)) continue;
        if (((flags & 0x8) && channel > 14) ||
            ((flags & 0x10) && channel <= 14)) continue;
        candidate.channelBitmap[channel / 8] |=
            static_cast<uint8_t>(1U << (channel % 8));
    }
    out = candidate;
    return true;
}

struct Join {
    uint32_t lowerAuth;
    uint32_t upperAuth;
    uint8_t ssid[32];
    uint32_t ssidLength;
    uint8_t key[64];
    uint32_t keyLength;
    uint32_t keyCipher;
    uint16_t keyIndex;
    uint8_t bssid[6];
    uint16_t channelSpec;
    uint8_t rsn[257];
    uint16_t rsnLength;
};

inline void wipeBytes(void *buffer, size_t length) {
    volatile uint8_t *bytes = static_cast<volatile uint8_t *>(buffer);
    while (length--) *bytes++ = 0;
}

inline bool validBSSID(const uint8_t bssid[6]) {
    uint8_t aggregate = 0;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 6; ++i) aggregate |= bssid[i];
    return aggregate != 0 && (bssid[0] & 1U) == 0;
}

inline bool validRSN(const uint8_t *ie, uint16_t length) {
    if (!length) return true;
    return ie && length >= 4 && ie[0] == 48 &&
           static_cast<uint16_t>(ie[1] + 2U) == length;
}

/* This RTW88 native-WCL slice supports open networks and WPA2-PSK with a
 * 32-byte PMK. Enterprise, SHA256 AKMs, passphrases, WEP and SAE are rejected
 * because the current RTW88 join engine has not implemented those WCL modes. */
inline bool supportedJoinAuth(const Join &j) {
    if (j.lowerAuth != 1 || !validRSN(j.rsn, j.rsnLength)) return false;
    if (j.upperAuth == 0)
        return j.keyLength == 0 && j.keyCipher == 0 && j.rsnLength == 0;
    return j.upperAuth == (1U << 3) && j.keyLength == 32 &&
           (j.keyCipher == 6 || j.keyCipher == 7);
}

inline bool decodeJoin(const void *buffer, size_t length, Join &out) {
    if (!buffer || length < 0x218) return false;
    const uint8_t *p = static_cast<const uint8_t *>(buffer);
    const uint32_t candidateCount = read32(p + 0x214);
    const uint32_t ssidLength = read32(p + 0x1c);
    const uint32_t keyLength = read32(p + 0x44);
    const uint16_t rsnLength = read16(p + 0xd4);
    if (candidateCount == 0 || candidateCount > 10 ||
        ssidLength == 0 || ssidLength > 32 || keyLength > 64 ||
        rsnLength > 257 || length < 0x218 + static_cast<size_t>(candidateCount) * 18 ||
        p[0x21c] & 1U) return false;
    if (keyLength > 32 && read32(p + 0x48) != 10) return false;

    Join candidate = {};
    candidate.lowerAuth = read32(p + 0x10);
    candidate.upperAuth = read32(p + 0x14);
    candidate.ssidLength = ssidLength;
    memcpy(candidate.ssid, p + 0x20, ssidLength);
    candidate.keyLength = keyLength;
    candidate.keyCipher = read32(p + 0x48);
    candidate.keyIndex = read16(p + 0x4e);
    memcpy(candidate.key, p + 0x50, keyLength);
    memcpy(candidate.bssid, p + 0x21c, 6);
    candidate.channelSpec = read16(p + 0x228);
    candidate.rsnLength = rsnLength;
    memcpy(candidate.rsn, p + 0xd6, rsnLength);
    if (!validBSSID(candidate.bssid) || !supportedJoinAuth(candidate)) {
        wipeBytes(candidate.key, sizeof(candidate.key));
        return false;
    }
    out = candidate;
    wipeBytes(candidate.key, sizeof(candidate.key));
    return true;
}
} // namespace RTW88WCLRequest
