/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Sequoia 24H32 Skywalk queue and packet-pool resource owner.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLPacketView.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.h>

/* The bundled SDK declares PoolOptions before its public: label. Expose the
 * exact protected nested ABI type through a derived access shim; its layout is
 * unchanged and no shim object is instantiated. */
class RTW88WCLPoolOptionsAccess : public IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool {
public:
    using PoolOptions = IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool::PoolOptions;
};

#define super OSObject
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues, OSObject)

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::init()
{
    if (!super::init())
        return false;
    _txPool = nullptr;
    _rxPool = nullptr;
    _txSubmission = nullptr;
    _txCompletion = nullptr;
    _rxSubmission = nullptr;
    _rxCompletion = nullptr;
    _allocated = false;
    return true;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::createQueues()
{
    if (_allocated)
        return kIOReturnSuccess;
    if (_txPool || _rxPool || _txSubmission || _txCompletion ||
        _rxSubmission || _rxCompletion)
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    RTW88WCLPoolOptionsAccess::PoolOptions txOptions = {};
    txOptions.packetCount = kQueueCapacity;
    txOptions.bufferCount = kQueueCapacity;
    txOptions.bufferSize = kTxBufferSize;
    txOptions.maxBuffersPerPacket = 1;
    txOptions.memorySegmentSize = kQueueCapacity * kTxBufferSize;
    txOptions.poolFlags = 0;
    txOptions._reserved = 0;

    RTW88WCLPoolOptionsAccess::PoolOptions rxOptions = txOptions;
    rxOptions.bufferSize = kRxBufferSize;
    rxOptions.memorySegmentSize = kQueueCapacity * kRxBufferSize;

    _txPool = IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool::withName(
        "RTL88WiFi-WCL-TX", this, kIOSkywalkPacketTypeNetwork, &txOptions);
    if (!_txPool)
        goto no_memory;

    _rxPool = IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool::withName(
        "RTL88WiFi-WCL-RX", this, kIOSkywalkPacketTypeNetwork, &rxOptions);
    if (!_rxPool)
        goto no_memory;

    /* These queue objects are deliberately not added to an IOWorkLoop and
     * are not passed to registerEthernetInterface() yet. Skywalk owns their
     * scheduling and packet lifetime only after a complete RegistrationInfo
     * + ring setup transaction, which is a later stage. */
    _txSubmission = IOSkywalkTxSubmissionQueue::withPool(
        _txPool, kQueueCapacity, 0, this,
        &RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::txSubmissionAction, this, 0);
    if (!_txSubmission)
        goto no_memory;

    _txCompletion = IOSkywalkTxCompletionQueue::withPool(
        _txPool, kQueueCapacity, 0, this,
        &RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::txCompletionAction, this, 0);
    if (!_txCompletion)
        goto no_memory;

    _rxSubmission = IOSkywalkRxSubmissionQueue::withPool(
        _rxPool, kQueueCapacity, 0, static_cast<kern_packet_svc_class_t>(0),
        this, &RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::rxSubmissionAction, this, 0);
    if (!_rxSubmission)
        goto no_memory;

    _rxCompletion = IOSkywalkRxCompletionQueue::withPool(
        _rxPool, kQueueCapacity, 0, this,
        &RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::rxCompletionAction, this, 0);
    if (!_rxCompletion)
        goto no_memory;

    _allocated = true;
    IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: allocated Skywalk pools and four queue objects (not registered)\n");
    return kIOReturnSuccess;

no_memory:
    destroyQueues();
    return kIOReturnNoMemory;
}

void RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::destroyQueues()
{
    /* Release queues before their pools: queue implementations retain and
     * reference their associated pool for packet and buffer accounting. */
    if (_rxCompletion) {
        _rxCompletion->release();
        _rxCompletion = nullptr;
    }
    if (_rxSubmission) {
        _rxSubmission->release();
        _rxSubmission = nullptr;
    }
    if (_txCompletion) {
        _txCompletion->release();
        _txCompletion = nullptr;
    }
    if (_txSubmission) {
        _txSubmission->release();
        _txSubmission = nullptr;
    }
    if (_rxPool) {
        _rxPool->release();
        _rxPool = nullptr;
    }
    if (_txPool) {
        _txPool->release();
        _txPool = nullptr;
    }
    _allocated = false;
}

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::queuesAllocated() const
{
    return _allocated && _txPool && _rxPool && _txSubmission &&
           _txCompletion && _rxSubmission && _rxCompletion;
}

/*
 * Sequoia 24H32 callback contract: although the bundled Skywalk action
 * typedefs are declared as IOReturn, the audited queue dispatchers
 * consume these callback return values as packet counts. Zero means
 * that no packets were accepted or completed. Do not return nonzero
 * IOReturn error constants from these action callbacks.
 */
IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::txSubmissionAction(
    OSObject *owner, IOSkywalkTxSubmissionQueue *queue,
    const IOSkywalkPacket **packets, UInt32 packetCount, void *refCon)
{
    (void)refCon;
    auto *self = OSDynamicCast(RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues, owner);
    if (!self || queue != self->_txSubmission || !packets || packetCount == 0)
        return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);

    /* Validate every descriptor before a future TX path is allowed to copy
     * bytes or hand ownership to RTW88. The callback remains fail-closed:
     * a validated packet is not yet accepted until its hardware completion
     * cookie is connected to the native completion queue. */
    for (UInt32 i = 0; i < packetCount; ++i) {
        if (!packets[i])
            return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);
        RTW88WCLPacketView view = {};
        if (!RTW88WCLResolveEthernetTxPacket(
                const_cast<IOSkywalkPacket *>(packets[i]), self->_txPool, &view))
            return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);
    }

    return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::txCompletionAction(
    OSObject *owner, IOSkywalkTxCompletionQueue *queue,
    IOSkywalkPacket **packets, UInt32 packetCount, void *refCon)
{
    (void)owner; (void)queue; (void)packets; (void)packetCount; (void)refCon;
    return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::rxSubmissionAction(
    OSObject *owner, IOSkywalkRxSubmissionQueue *queue,
    const IOSkywalkPacket **packets, UInt32 packetCount, void *refCon)
{
    (void)owner; (void)queue; (void)packets; (void)packetCount; (void)refCon;
    /* RX buffer ownership/refill is intentionally not accepted yet. */
    return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::rxCompletionAction(
    OSObject *owner, IOSkywalkRxCompletionQueue *queue,
    IOSkywalkPacket **packets, UInt32 packetCount, void *refCon)
{
    (void)owner; (void)queue; (void)packets; (void)packetCount; (void)refCon;
    return static_cast<IOReturn>(0);
}

void RTW88WCLSkywalkQueues::free()
{
    destroyQueues();
    super::free();
}

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Sequoia Skywalk queue/pool resource owner.
 *
 * This stage allocates the native queue objects and packet pools but does not
 * register them with IOSkywalkEthernetInterface. Queue callbacks intentionally
 * fail closed until packet layout, completion semantics and RX buffer refill
 * have been validated against the 24H32 runtime.
 */

public:
    bool init() override;
    IOReturn createQueues();
    void destroyQueues();
    bool queuesAllocated() const;
    void free() override;

    static constexpr UInt32 kQueueCapacity = 128;
    static constexpr UInt32 kTxBufferSize = 2048;
    static constexpr UInt32 kRxBufferSize = 4096;

    /* Borrowed by the controller only long enough for the RX adapter to retain it. */
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *rxPacketPool() const { return _rxPool; }

private:
    static IOReturn txSubmissionAction(OSObject *owner,
                                      IOSkywalkTxSubmissionQueue *queue,
                                      const IOSkywalkPacket **packets,
                                      UInt32 packetCount, void *refCon);
    static IOReturn txCompletionAction(OSObject *owner,
                                       IOSkywalkTxCompletionQueue *queue,

                                       IOSkywalkRxCompletionQueue *queue,
                                       IOSkywalkPacket **packets,
                                       UInt32 packetCount, void *refCon);

    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *_txPool = nullptr;
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *_rxPool = nullptr;
    IOSkywalkTxSubmissionQueue *_txSubmission = nullptr;
    IOSkywalkTxCompletionQueue *_txCompletion = nullptr;
    IOSkywalkRxSubmissionQueue *_rxSubmission = nullptr;
    IOSkywalkRxCompletionQueue *_rxCompletion = nullptr;

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Deferred bounded RX conversion. Packet delivery stays disabled until the
 * 24H32 registration and RX-completion ownership contract is verified.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLPacketSpanValidation.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkMemorySegment.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkNetworkPacket.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkPacketBuffer.h>
#include <sys/kpi_mbuf.h>

#define super IOEventSource
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter, IOEventSource)

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::init(OSObject *owner, IOWorkLoop *workLoop,
                                    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *rxPool,
                                    FrameSink sink, void *context)
{
    if (!owner || !workLoop || !rxPool || _workLoop || _rxPool || _registered)
        return false;
    if (!super::init(owner, nullptr))
        return false;

    _frames.reset();
    _workLoop = workLoop;
    _rxPool = rxPool;
    _rxPool->retain();
    _sink = sink;
    _sinkContext = context;
    __atomic_store_n(&_pendingSignal, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    __atomic_store_n(&_stopping, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    __atomic_store_n(&_dropped, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
    __atomic_store_n(&_prepared, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);

    const IOReturn status = workLoop->addEventSource(this);
    if (status != kIOReturnSuccess) {
        _rxPool->release();
        _rxPool = nullptr;
        _workLoop = nullptr;
        _sink = nullptr;
        _sinkContext = nullptr;
        return false;
    }
    _registered = true;
    return true;
}

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::isValidMbuf(mbuf_t packet)
{
    if (!packet)
        return false;

    /* Do not require the Ethernet header to fit in the first mbuf. RTW88 may
     * hand us a chained mbuf; mbuf_copydata() below safely spans the chain. */
    const size_t packetLength = mbuf_pkthdr_len(packet);
    return packetLength >= kMinimumFrameLength &&
           packetLength <= kMaximumFrameLength;
}

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::copyMbufToSkywalkPacket(
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *pool, mbuf_t source,
    IOSkywalkPacket **outPacket)
{
    if (outPacket)
        *outPacket = nullptr;
    if (!pool || !source || !outPacket)
        return false;

    const size_t packetLength = mbuf_pkthdr_len(source);
    if (packetLength < kMinimumFrameLength ||
        packetLength > kMaximumFrameLength)
        return false;

    IOSkywalkPacket *packet = nullptr;
    /* Option bit 0 requests non-blocking allocation in a packet callback. */
    if (pool->allocatePacket(1, &packet, 1) != kIOReturnSuccess || !packet)
        return false;

    bool valid = false;
    do {
        if (packet->getPacketType() != kIOSkywalkPacketTypeNetwork ||
            packet->getPacketBufferPool() != pool ||
            packet->getPacketBufferCount() != 1)
            break;

        auto *networkPacket = OSDynamicCast(IOSkywalkNetworkPacket, packet);
        if (!networkPacket)
            break;

        IOSkywalkPacketBuffer *buffers[1] = { nullptr };
        if (packet->getPacketBuffers(buffers, 1) != 1 || !buffers[0])
            break;
        IOSkywalkPacketBuffer *buffer = buffers[0];
        if (buffer->getPacketBufferPool() != pool)
            break;

        IOSkywalkMemorySegment *segment = buffer->getMemorySegment();
        if (!segment)
            break;
        IOMemoryDescriptor *memory = segment->getMemoryDescriptor();
        if (!memory)
            break;

        const uint64_t segmentBase = segment->getVirtualAddress();
        const RTW88WCLPacketSpan span = {
            memory->getLength(),
            buffer->getMemorySegmentOffset(),
            buffer->getBufferSize(),
            0,
            static_cast<uint32_t>(packetLength)
        };
        uintptr_t destinationAddress = 0;
        if (!RTW88WCLResolvePacketBufferAddress(
                segmentBase, span, &destinationAddress))
            break;

        if (packet->setDataOffsetAndLength(
                0, static_cast<UInt32>(packetLength)) != kIOReturnSuccess)
            break;
        if (packet->getDataOffset() != 0 ||
            packet->getDataLength() != packetLength ||
            buffer->getDataOffset() != 0 ||
            buffer->getDataLength() != packetLength)
            break;

        auto *destination = reinterpret_cast<void *>(destinationAddress);
        if (mbuf_copydata(source, 0, packetLength, destination) != 0)
            break;

        if (networkPacket->setCompletionStatus(0) != kIOReturnSuccess)
            break;

        *outPacket = packet;
        valid = true;
    } while (false);

    if (!valid)
        (void)pool->deallocatePacket(packet);
    return valid;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::enqueueFrame(mbuf_t packet)
{
    if (!packet)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    if (__atomic_load_n(&_stopping, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) ||
        !isValidMbuf(packet)) {
        __atomic_add_fetch(&_dropped, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        mbuf_freem(packet);
        return kIOReturnNotReady;
    }

    if (!_frames.push(packet)) {
        __atomic_add_fetch(&_dropped, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        mbuf_freem(packet);
        return kIOReturnNoResources;
    }

    uint32_t expected = 0;
    if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_pendingSignal, &expected, 1U, false,
                                    __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
        signalWorkAvailable();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::checkForWork()
{
    if (__atomic_load_n(&_stopping, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
        return false;

    uint32_t drained = 0;
    mbuf_t source = nullptr;
    while (drained < kDrainBudget && _frames.pop(&source)) {
        if (source) {
            IOSkywalkPacket *packet = nullptr;
            if (copyMbufToSkywalkPacket(_rxPool, source, &packet)) {
                __atomic_add_fetch(&_prepared, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
                /* A true return means the sink has accepted ownership. The
                 * current controller sink returns false because the RX
                 * completion queue is still unregistered. */
                const bool accepted = _sink && _sink(packet, _sinkContext);
                if (!accepted) {
                    (void)_rxPool->deallocatePacket(packet);
                    __atomic_add_fetch(&_dropped, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
                }
            } else {
                __atomic_add_fetch(&_dropped, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
            }
            mbuf_freem(source);
            source = nullptr;
        }
        ++drained;
    }

    if (drained == kDrainBudget)
        return true; // bounded workloop occupancy; schedule another pass

    __atomic_store_n(&_pendingSignal, 0U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    if (!_frames.empty()) {
        uint32_t expected = 0;
        if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&_pendingSignal, &expected, 1U,
                                        false, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL,
                                        __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
            return true;
    }
    return false;
}

void RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::stop()
{
    __atomic_store_n(&_stopping, 1U, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    if (_registered && _workLoop) {
        /* Synchronous removal serializes against checkForWork(). The owner
         * must stop/quiesce RTW88 RX producers before invoking stop(). */
        _workLoop->removeEventSource(this);
        _registered = false;
    }

    mbuf_t packet = nullptr;
    while (_frames.pop(&packet)) {
        if (packet)
            mbuf_freem(packet);
        packet = nullptr;
    }
    _sink = nullptr;
    _sinkContext = nullptr;
    _workLoop = nullptr;
    if (_rxPool) {
        _rxPool->release();
        _rxPool = nullptr;
    }
}

void RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::free()
{
    stop();
    super::free();
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::droppedFrames() const
{
    return __atomic_load_n(&_dropped, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::preparedFrames() const
{
    return __atomic_load_n(&_prepared, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter::queuedFrames() const
{
    return _frames.approximateSize();
}

#undef super

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Bounded RTW88 mbuf staging and native Skywalk RX packet construction.
 */
#pragma once

#include <IOKit/IOEventSource.h>
#include <IOKit/IOWorkLoop.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkPacket.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSObject.h>
#include <sys/mbuf.h>

#include "RTW88WCLMbufRing.hpp"

class RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter final : public IOEventSource {
    OSDeclareDefaultStructors(RTW88WCLSkywalkRxAdapter)

public:
    /* true transfers the prepared packet to the sink; false returns it to RX pool. */
    using FrameSink = bool (*)(IOSkywalkPacket *preparedPacket, void *context);
    static constexpr uint32_t kCapacity = 128;
    static constexpr uint32_t kDrainBudget = 32;
    static constexpr uint32_t kMinimumFrameLength = 14;
    static constexpr uint32_t kMaximumFrameLength = 4096;

    bool init(OSObject *owner, IOWorkLoop *workLoop,
              IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *rxPool,
              FrameSink sink, void *context);

    /* Consumes mbuf ownership on every return path. The producer path performs
     * only bounded validation/enqueue and a workloop signal. */
    IOReturn enqueueFrame(mbuf_t packet);

    /* Removes the event source, drains queued mbufs, then releases the retained pool. */
    void stop();
    void free() override;

    bool checkForWork() override;
    uint32_t droppedFrames() const;
    uint32_t preparedFrames() const;
    uint32_t queuedFrames() const;
    bool isRegistered() const { return _registered; }

private:
    static bool isValidMbuf(mbuf_t packet);
    static bool copyMbufToSkywalkPacket(IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *pool,
                                        mbuf_t source,
                                        IOSkywalkPacket **outPacket);

    RTW88WCLMbufRing<mbuf_t, kCapacity> _frames;
    IOWorkLoop *_workLoop = nullptr; // non-owning; controller owns workloop
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *_rxPool = nullptr; // retained
    FrameSink _sink = nullptr;       // non-owning; invoked on workloop
    void *_sinkContext = nullptr;
    volatile uint32_t _pendingSignal = 0;
    volatile uint32_t _stopping = 0;
    volatile uint32_t _dropped = 0;
    volatile uint32_t _prepared = 0;
    bool _registered = false;        // workloop lifecycle-owned
};

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Sequoia 24H32 Skywalk Ethernet TX packet conversion and completion bridge.
 */
#include "RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter.hpp"
#include "RTW88WCLPacketView.hpp"
#include "../RTW88IEEE80211.hpp"

#include <IOKit/IOLib.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSMetaClass.h>
#include <sys/kpi_mbuf.h>

#define super OSObject
OSDefineMetaClassAndStructors(RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter, OSObject)

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::init()
{
    if (!super::init())
        return false;
    _radio = nullptr;
    _txPool = nullptr;
    _tickets.reset();
    return true;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::bind(
    RTW88IEEE80211 *radio, IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *txPool)
{
    if (!radio || !txPool)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (_radio || _txPool || _tickets.hasOutstanding())
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    radio->retain();
    txPool->retain();
    _radio = radio;
    _txPool = txPool;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::unbind()
{
    if (_tickets.hasOutstanding())
        return kIOReturnBusy;

    RTW88IEEE80211 *radio = _radio;
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *pool = _txPool;
    _radio = nullptr;
    _txPool = nullptr;
    if (pool)
        pool->release();
    if (radio)
        radio->release();
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

mbuf_t RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::copyPacketToMbuf(
    const uint8_t *bytes, uint32_t length)
{
    if (!bytes || length < 14 || length > 2048)
        return nullptr;

    mbuf_t mbuf = nullptr;
    if (mbuf_allocpacket(MBUF_DONTWAIT, length, nullptr, &mbuf) != 0 || !mbuf)
        return nullptr;

    /* allocpacket may return a chain; copyback handles every segment. Keep
     * the copy non-blocking because this runs in a Skywalk queue callback. */
    if (mbuf_copyback(mbuf, 0, length, bytes, MBUF_DONTWAIT) != 0) {
        mbuf_freem(mbuf);
        return nullptr;
    }
    mbuf_pkthdr_setlen(mbuf, length);
    return mbuf;
}

IOReturn RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::submitPacket(IOSkywalkPacket *packet)
{
    if (!packet)
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;
    if (!_radio || !_txPool)
        return kIOReturnNotReady;

    RTW88WCLPacketView view = {};
    if (!RTW88WCLResolveEthernetTxPacket(packet, _txPool, &view))
        return kIOReturnBadArgument;

    mbuf_t mbuf = copyPacketToMbuf(view.bytes, view.length);
    if (!mbuf)
        return kIOReturnNoMemory;

    packet->retain();
    TicketPool::Ticket *ticket = _tickets.reserve(packet);
    if (!ticket) {
        packet->release();
        mbuf_freem(mbuf);
        return kIOReturnBusy;
    }

    /* Keep the adapter alive until the workloop-side completion drain drops
     * this ticket. backendCompletion() intentionally does not release it. */
    retain();
    const UInt32 backendResult = _radio->outputPacketWithCompletion(
        mbuf, &RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::backendCompletion, this, ticket);

    /* outputPacketWithCompletion consumes the mbuf and reports exactly one
     * terminal callback, including synchronous drops. Once the ticket has
     * been reserved, adapter acceptance is successful; the actual transmitted
     * vs dropped result is delivered later through the completion sink. */
    (void)backendResult;
    return kIOReturnSuccess;
}

void RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::backendCompletion(
    void *context, void *cookie, bool transmitted)
{
    auto *self = static_cast<RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter *>(context);
    auto *ticket = static_cast<TicketPool::Ticket *>(cookie);
    if (!self || !ticket)
        return;

    /* No Skywalk API, OSObject release, allocation, or blocking operation is
     * allowed here. A deferred consumer performs the completion handoff. */
    (void)TicketPool::complete(ticket, transmitted);
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::drainCompletions(
    CompletionSink sink, void *context)
{
    if (!sink)
        return 0;

    /* A sink may trigger owner teardown; keep this adapter alive until the
     * drain loop has stopped touching its ticket pool. */
    retain();
    uint32_t drained = 0;
    for (;;) {
        TicketPool::Ticket *ticket = _tickets.claimCompleted();
        if (!ticket)
            break;

        auto *packet = static_cast<IOSkywalkPacket *>(TicketPool::packet(ticket));
        const bool transmitted = TicketPool::transmitted(ticket);

        /* The sink runs in deferred context and borrows the retained packet.
         * It must finish any Skywalk queue handoff before returning. */
        if (packet)
            sink(packet, transmitted, context);

        TicketPool::finish(ticket);
        if (packet)
            packet->release();
        release();
        ++drained;
    }
    release();
    return drained;
}

bool RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::hasOutstandingCompletions() const
{
    return _tickets.hasOutstanding();
}

uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::outstandingCompletionCount() const
{
    return _tickets.outstandingCount();
}

void RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::free()
{
    /* A per-ticket retain prevents free() while hardware callbacks are still
     * pending. The owning controller must drain every completion before its
     * final reference is released. */
    if (_tickets.hasOutstanding())
        IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: TX adapter free attempted with outstanding tickets\n");
    unbind();
    super::free();
}

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Skywalk -> mbuf -> RTW88 TX adapter, isolated until queue registration.
 */
#pragma once

#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkPacket.h>
#include <IOKit/skywalk/IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool.h>
#include <libkern/c++/OSObject.h>
#include <sys/mbuf.h>

#include "RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots.hpp"

class RTW88IEEE80211;

class RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter final : public OSObject {
    OSDeclareDefaultStructors(RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter)

public:
    using CompletionSink = void (*)(IOSkywalkPacket *packet,
                                    bool transmitted,
                                    void *context);

    static constexpr uint32_t kCompletionCapacity = 128;

    bool init() override;
    IOReturn bind(RTW88IEEE80211 *radio,
                  IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *txPool);
    IOReturn unbind();

    /* Copies a validated, single-buffer Skywalk Ethernet frame to an mbuf,
     * then submits it to RTW88 with a bounded completion ticket. Ownership of
     * the Skywalk packet stays with this adapter until drainCompletions(). */
    IOReturn submitPacket(IOSkywalkPacket *packet);

    /* Must be called later from a serialized/deferred context. The sink may
     * enqueue the packet to Skywalk's TX-completion queue once that queue's
     * ABI and lifecycle have been validated. The adapter releases its packet
     * retain after the sink returns. */
    uint32_t drainCompletions(CompletionSink sink, void *context);

    bool hasOutstandingCompletions() const;
    uint32_t outstandingCompletionCount() const;
    void free() override;

private:
    using TicketPool = RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots<kCompletionCapacity>;

    static void backendCompletion(void *context, void *cookie,
                                  bool transmitted);
    static mbuf_t copyPacketToMbuf(const uint8_t *bytes, uint32_t length);

    RTW88IEEE80211 *_radio = nullptr; // retained while bound
    IOSkywalkPacketBufferPool *_txPool = nullptr; // retained while bound
    TicketPool _tickets;
};


=== FILE driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots.hpp SHA256=af7a498d3b5d2e4855609533bd7b823e40a5ef6c83e27c76abfe2e497f7c8f7f BYTES=4323 ===

/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
 * Fixed-capacity completion tickets for the native WCL TX adapter.
 *
 * Multiple RTW88 completion contexts may publish concurrently. The Skywalk
 * packet operations are deliberately kept out of complete(); a workloop-side
 * consumer claims completed tickets and performs the deferred queue handoff.
 */
#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>

/* Freestanding so the state machine can be stress-tested outside the kernel. */
template <uint32_t Capacity>
class RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots {
    static_assert(Capacity > 0, "completion slot capacity must be non-zero");

public:
    enum : uint32_t {
        kFree = 0,
        kReserved,
        kSubmitted,
        kCompleting,
        kCompleted,
        kDraining
    };

    struct Ticket {
        volatile uint32_t state;
        void *packet;
        uint32_t transmitted;
    };

    void reset()
    {
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
            _slots[i].packet = nullptr;
            _slots[i].transmitted = 0;
            __atomic_store_n(&_slots[i].state, kFree, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        }
    }

    /* Called before handing a packet to RTW88. The caller owns the packet
     * retain associated with the returned ticket. */
    Ticket *reserve(void *packet)
    {
        if (!packet)
            return nullptr;

        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
            Ticket *ticket = &_slots[i];
            uint32_t expected = kFree;
            if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&ticket->state, &expected,
                                             kReserved, false,
                                             __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL,
                                             __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
                continue;

            ticket->packet = packet;
            ticket->transmitted = 0;
            __atomic_store_n(&ticket->state, kSubmitted, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
            return ticket;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    /* Safe for the RTW88 completion callback: bounded atomics only, no
     * allocation, locks, Skywalk calls, or packet release. */
    static bool complete(Ticket *ticket, bool transmitted)
    {
        if (!ticket)
            return false;
        uint32_t expected = kSubmitted;
        if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&ticket->state, &expected,
                                         kCompleting, false,
                                         __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL,
                                         __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
            return false;
        ticket->transmitted = transmitted ? 1U : 0U;
        __atomic_store_n(&ticket->state, kCompleted, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
        return true;
    }

    /* Single consumer, called from the adapter's deferred/workloop context. */
    Ticket *claimCompleted()
    {
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
            Ticket *ticket = &_slots[i];
            uint32_t expected = kCompleted;
            if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&ticket->state, &expected,
                                            kDraining, false,
                                            __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL,
                                            __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
                return ticket;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    static void finish(Ticket *ticket)
    {
        if (!ticket)
            return;
        ticket->packet = nullptr;
        ticket->transmitted = 0;
        __atomic_store_n(&ticket->state, kFree, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    }

    static void *packet(const Ticket *ticket)
    {
        return ticket ? ticket->packet : nullptr;
    }

    static bool transmitted(const Ticket *ticket)
    {
        return ticket && ticket->transmitted != 0;
    }

    bool hasOutstanding() const
    {
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
            if (__atomic_load_n(&_slots[i].state, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != kFree)
                return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    uint32_t outstandingCount() const
    {
        uint32_t count = 0;
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
            if (__atomic_load_n(&_slots[i].state, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != kFree)
                ++count;
        }
        return count;
    }

    static constexpr uint32_t capacity() { return Capacity; }

private:
    Ticket _slots[Capacity] = {};
};


=== FILE driver/rtw88-stable/drivers/net/wireless/realtek/rtw88/coex.c SHA256=3b7def2889fd75382b780437a8d0884f711efbde2961443ecdd6cd91f0353d42 BYTES=116630 ===

// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause
/* Copyright(c) 2018-2019  Realtek Corporation
 */

#include "main.h"
#include "coex.h"
#include "fw.h"
#include "ps.h"
#include "debug.h"
#include "reg.h"
#include "phy.h"

static u8 rtw_coex_next_rssi_state(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, u8 pre_state,
				   u8 rssi, u8 rssi_thresh)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	u8 tol = chip->rssi_tolerance;
	u8 next_state;

	if (pre_state == COEX_RSSI_STATE_LOW ||
	    pre_state == COEX_RSSI_STATE_STAY_LOW) {
		if (rssi >= (rssi_thresh + tol))
			next_state = COEX_RSSI_STATE_HIGH;
		else
			next_state = COEX_RSSI_STATE_STAY_LOW;
	} else {
		if (rssi < rssi_thresh)
			next_state = COEX_RSSI_STATE_LOW;
		else
			next_state = COEX_RSSI_STATE_STAY_HIGH;
	}

	return next_state;
}

static void rtw_coex_limited_tx(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev,
				bool tx_limit_en, bool ampdu_limit_en)
{
	const struct rtw_chip_info *chip = rtwdev->chip;
	struct rtw_coex *coex = &rtwdev->coex;
	struct rtw_coex_stat *coex_stat = &coex->stat;
	u8 num_of_active_port = 1;

	if (!chip->scbd_support)
		return;

	/* force max tx retry limit = 8 */
	if (coex_stat->wl_tx_limit_en == tx_limit_en &&
	    coex_stat->wl_ampdu_limit_en == ampdu_limit_en)
		return;

	if (!coex_stat->wl_tx_limit_en) {
		coex_stat->darfrc = rtw_read32(rtwdev, REG_DARFRC);
		coex_stat->darfrch = rtw_read32(rtwdev, REG_DARFRCH);
		coex_stat->retry_limit = rtw_read16(rtwdev, REG_RETRY_LIMIT);
	}

	if (!coex_stat->wl_ampdu_limit_en)
		coex_stat->ampdu_max_time =
				rtw_read8(rtwdev, REG_AMPDU_MAX_TIME_V1);

	coex_stat->wl_tx_limit_en = tx_limit_en;
	coex_stat->wl_ampdu_limit_en = ampdu_limit_en;

	if (tx_limit_en) {
		/* set BT polluted packet on for tx rate adaptive,
		 * not including tx retry broken by PTA
		 */
		rtw_write8_set(rtwdev, REG_TX_HANG_CTRL, BIT_EN_GNT_BT_AWAKE);

		/* set queue life time to avoid can't reach tx retry limit
		 * if tx is always broken by GNT_BT
		 */
		if (num_of_active_port <= 1)
			rtw_write8_set(rtwdev, REG_LIFETIME_EN, 0xf);
		rtw_write16(rtwdev, REG_RETRY_LIMIT, 0x0808);

		/* auto rate fallback step within 8 retries */
		rtw_write32(rtwdev, REG_DARFRC, 0x1000000);
		rtw_write32(rtwdev, REG_DARFRCH, 0x4030201);
	} else {
		rtw_write8_clr(rtwdev, REG_TX_HANG_CTRL, BIT_EN_GNT_BT_AWAKE);
		rtw_write8_clr(rtwdev, REG_LIFETIME_EN, 0xf);

		rtw_write16(rtwdev, REG_RETRY_LIMIT, coex_stat->retry_limit);
		rtw_write32(rtwdev, REG_DARFRC, coex_stat->darfrc);
		rtw_write32(rtwdev, REG_DARFRCH, coex_stat->darfrch);
	}

	if (ampdu_limit_en)
		rtw_write8(rtwdev, REG_AMPDU_MAX_TIME_V1, 0x20);
	else
		rtw_write8(rtwdev, REG_AMPDU_MAX_TIME_V1,
			   coex_stat->ampdu_max_time);
}

static void rtw_coex_limited_wl(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev)
{
	struct rtw_coex *coex = &rtwdev->coex;
	struct rtw_coex_dm *coex_dm = &coex->dm;
	bool tx_limit = false;
	bool tx_agg_ctrl = false;

	if (!coex->under_5g && coex_dm->bt_status != COEX_BTSTATUS_NCON_IDLE) {
		tx_limit = true;
		tx_agg_ctrl = true;
	}

	rtw_coex_limited_tx(rtwdev, tx_limit, tx_agg_ctrl);
}

static bool rtw_coex_freerun_check(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev)
{
	struct rtw_coex *coex = &rtwdev->coex;
	struct rtw_coex_dm *coex_dm = &coex->dm;
	struct rtw_coex_stat *coex_stat = &coex->stat;
	struct rtw_efuse *efuse = &rtwdev->efuse;
	u8 bt_rssi;
	u8 ant_distance = 10;

	if (coex_stat->bt_disabled)
		return false;

	if (efuse->share_ant || ant_distance <= 5 || !coex_stat->wl_gl_busy)
		return false;

	if (ant_distance >= 40 || coex_stat->bt_hid_pair_num >= 2)
		return true;

	/* ant_distance = 5 ~ 40  */
	if (COEX_RSSI_HIGH(coex_dm->wl_rssi_state[1]) &&
	    COEX_RSSI_HIGH(coex_dm->bt_rssi_state[0]))
		return true;

	if (coex_stat->wl_tput_dir == COEX_WL_TPUT_TX)
		bt_rssi = coex_dm->bt_rssi_state[0];
	else
		bt_rssi = coex_dm->bt_rssi_state[1];

	if (COEX_RSSI_HIGH(coex_dm->wl_rssi_state[3]) &&
	    COEX_RSSI_HIGH(bt_rssi) &&
	    coex_stat->cnt_wl[COEX_CNT_WL_SCANAP] <= 5)
		return true;

	return false;
}

static void rtw_coex_wl_slot_extend(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev, bool enable)
{
	struct rtw_coex *coex = &rtwdev->coex;
	struct rtw_coex_stat *coex_stat = &coex->stat;
	u8 para[6] = {0};

	para[0] = COEX_H2C69_WL_LEAKAP;
	para[1] = PARA1_H2C69_DIS_5MS;

	if (enable)
		para[1] = PARA1_H2C69_EN_5MS;
	else
		coex_stat->cnt_wl[COEX_CNT_WL_5MS_NOEXTEND] = 0;

	coex_stat->wl_slot_extend = enable;
	rtw_fw_bt_wifi_control(rtwdev, para[0], &para[1]);
}

static void rtw_coex_wl_ccklock_action(struct rtw_dev *rtwdev)
{
	struct rtw_coex *coex = &rtwdev->coex;
	struct rtw_coex_stat *coex_stat = &coex->stat;

	if (coex->manual_control || coex->stop_dm)
		return;


	if (coex_stat->tdma_timer_base == 3 && coex_stat->wl_slot_extend) {
		rtw_dbg(rtwdev, RTW_DBG_COEX,
			"[BTCoex], set h2c 0x69 opcode 12 to turn off 5ms WL slot extend!!\n");
		rtw_coex_wl_slot_extend(rtwdev, false);
		return;
	}

	if (coex_stat->wl_slot_extend && coex_stat->wl_force_lps_ctrl &&
	    !coex_stat->wl_cck_lock_ever) {
		if (coex_stat->wl_fw_dbg_info[7] <= 5)
			coex_stat->cnt_wl[COEX_CNT_WL_5MS_NOEXTEND]++;
		else
			coex_stat->cnt_wl[COEX_CNT_WL_5MS_NOEXTEND] = 0;

		rtw_dbg(rtwdev, RTW_DBG_COEX,
			"[BTCoex], 5ms WL slot extend cnt = %d!!\n",
			coex_stat->cnt_wl[COEX_CNT_WL_5MS_NOEXTEND]);

		if (coex_stat->cnt_wl[COEX_CNT_WL_5MS_NOEXTEND] == 7) {
			rtw_dbg(rtwdev, RTW_DBG_COEX,
				"[BTCoex], set h2c 0x69 opcode 12 to turn off 5ms WL slot extend!!\n");
			rtw_coex_wl_slot_extend(rtwdev, false);
		}
	} else if (!coex_stat->wl_slot_extend && coex_stat->wl_cck_lock) {
		rtw_dbg(rtwdev, RTW
